ṭēviṭ liviṅsṭaṉ
 mutaṟ patippu - 1946
innūl 2003il cāratā māṇikkam patippakam, ceṉṉi - 43, veḷiyiṭṭa patippi mūlamākak koṇṭu veḷivarukiṟatu.

mukavuri
‘eṉ kaṭaṉ paṇiceytu kiṭappatē’ eṉpatu appar cuvāmikaḷ aruḷ vākku. ataṉpaṭi potumakkaḷ nalattiyē nāṭi uḻittu tam nalatti iḻanta perumakkaḷ palarāvar. avarkaḷit ‘tiyāka puruṣar’ eṉṟu collalām. tam nāṭṭu makkaḷukkut toṇṭu ceyvativiṭa vēṟṟu nāṭu ceṉṟu, anākarika makkaḷuṭaṉ vāḻntu avarkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttalil tam vāḻnāḷik kaḻittal arumi
yiṉum arumi! perumiyiṉum perumi!! tamakku naṉmi
ceyya vantavar eṉpati aṟiyātu appāmara makkaḷ apperumakkaḷik kollavum tuṇivar. perumakkaḷ avarkaḷi maṉṉittu avarkaḷōṭu eḷiya vāḻkki vāḻntu avarkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttuvatilēyē nāṭṭaṅ
koṇṭaṉar eṉiṉ, ap perumakkaḷ pūjikkattakkavarē āvar.
attakiya perumakkaḷil ṭēviṭ liviṅsṭaṉ oruvar. ivaratu tiyāka vāḻvu nam māṇavarkku ōr muṉ mātiriyākum. ‘intiyāvukku ittakiya tiyāka puruṣar palar tēvippaṭukiṉṟaṉar’, eṉṟa vivēkāṉantar tiruvākku cintikkattakkatu.
poruḷaṭakkam
mukavuri	3
1. muṉṉuri	5
2. kuṭumpamum piṟappum	8
3. iḷami : kalviyil ārvam	12
4. uyar tarak kalviyum paṇiyēṟpum	16
5. teṉ āḵprikā cellal	18
6. kurumāṉ paṇiniliyam	22
7. arimā vēṭṭi	27
8. maṇa vāḻkki	33
9. seccī° maṉamāṟṟam	35
10. kalahārip pālivaṉam	39
11. iraṇṭām pirayāṇam	45
12. kiḻakku āḵprikā	54
13. mūṉṟām pirayāṇam	60
14. liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ maṟivu	67
15. liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ aruṅkuṇaṅkaḷ	72
1. muṉṉuri
iṉpanili
makkaḷākap piṟantavar ellōrum iṉpattiyum veṟṟiyiyumē nāṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, iṉpattiṉ uyir
nili tuṉpattiṟkuḷ maṟintu kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpatiyum, veṟṟiyiṉ tiṟavukōl tōlvikaḷ eṉṟa peṭṭakaṅ
kaḷilēyē putiyuṇṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatiyum aṟintavar mikac cilarēyāvar. itaṉālēyē tiruvaḷḷuvar perumāṉ, “iṉpatti virumpāmal tuṉpattiyē iyalpeṉṟu mēṟkoḷpavaṉ uṇmiyil tuṉpatti aṭiva
tilli” eṉṟa poruḷuṭaṉ, 
“iṉpam viḻiyāṉ iṭumpi iyalpeṉpāṉ
  tuṉpam uṟutal ilaṉ”
eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟār. 
periyavar - ciṟiyar
tuṉpaṅkaḷi varavēṟṟu avaṟṟi etirttup pōrāṭuvataṟku vēṇṭum uṭalvaliyum maṉavaliyum  iḷamiyilēyē peritum kāṇappaṭum. iḷamiyil tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum tōlvikaḷiyum kaṇṭu añcupavarum, anta  accattiṉāl ūkkam iḻappavarum vāḻkkiyil aruñ ceyalkaḷic cātiyār. avar nāḷaṭivil ciṟumippaṭṭut tam vāḻvil veṟuppu aṭivar. mēlum avar tam vāḻkkiyiṉ pōkku muḻuvatum tam kiyil illi eṉṟum, viṉippayaṉ allatu talivitiyiṉ paṭiyē ellām naṭakkum eṉṟum eṇṇuvar. āṉāl, vāḻkkiyil veṟṟi peṟṟōr aṉi varum tamviti eṉpatu, tammic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cūḻnilikaḷum tam paḻiya muyaṟciyiṉmiyiṉ payaṉumē aṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu eṉṟa aṟivar. avar avviraṇṭiyum muyaṟciyāl vellalām eṉṟa uṇarntu mēṉmēlum muyalvar.  iṅṅaṉam periya kāriyaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭṭup peru muyaṟciyāl veṟṟi peṟṟavarkaḷik kuṟittē tiruvaḷḷuvar,
“ceyaṟkariya ceyvār periyar; ciṟiyar
  ceyaṟkariya ceykalātār”
eṉṟu pakarntār. 
periyōr tām ceyyum peru muyaṟcikaḷ cilavaṟṟil iṟuti veṟṟi kāṇātu pōtalum uṇṭu. eṉiṉum ulakattār, peruntaṉmi mikka  attakiyavar tōlvi
kaḷic ciṟiyōrkaḷiṉ ciṟu veṟṟikaḷiviṭap paṉmaṭaṅku uyarvu uṭiyatākap pōṟṟuvar.
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ
iṅṅaṉam ceyaṟkariya ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭup pukaḻ eytiya ulakap periyār palar. avaruḷ ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ1 eṉṟa āṅkilap periyār oruvar. ivar iḷami yilēyē ceyaṟkariya peruṅkāriyaṅkaḷic ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu maṉattil uṟuti koṇṭavar, tam āṟṟaliyum aṟiviyum piṟar nammikkākavum piṟar iṉpattukkākavum īṭupaṭutti uḻittavar. atumaṭṭumaṉṟu. ivar nāṭiya naṉmi tam nāṭṭiyō, tam iṉattiyō tam pōṉṟa uyarniliyiṉariyō paṟṟiyataṉṟu. nāṭukaḷil tāḻttappaṭṭa nāṭāy, iṉaṅkaḷil cīraḻivuṟṟa iṉamāy, nākarika ulakil maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaṉ ottu muṉṉēṟa muṭiyāmal niṉṟirunta āḵprikka nāṭṭu makkaḷ nalaṉukkē ivar pāṭupaṭṭār. ataṟkākat tam vāḻkki nalaṉ muḻumiyiyum tuṟantu uḻittār. 
aruñceyal
oruvar tam nalam tuṟantu tam vakuppu nalaṉ, tam nāṭṭu nalaṉ ākiyavaṟṟiṟkāka uḻippatē ulakil perumi
yuṭiya ceyalākak koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē tam nāṭu, tam iṉam ākiya ellikaḷik kaṭantu nākarika makkaḷ cellāta aṭarnta kāṭukaḷilum pālivaṉaṅ
kaḷilum tirintu, avarkaḷ neruṅkavum añcum muraṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē liviṅ°kiṉ toṇṭāṟṟa muṉvantatu evvaḷavu uyarvuṭiya ceyal eṉṟu kūṟāmalē viḷaṅkum aṉṟō?
aṭikkuṟippu
1.	ṉuyeṉi ṭuieipēḷavaṭinē
2. kuṭumpamum piṟappum
°kāṭlāntu
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ °kāṭlāntu1 nāṭṭiṉar. an
nāṭu kallum maliyum kāṭṭāṟum niṟintatu. iṭiyiṭiyē āṅkāṅku ērikaḷ kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaṉ ilaṅkum. an nāṭṭiṉ kaṭaṟkari amippu piṟa nāṭukaḷip pōlaṉṟik kuṟukkum neṭukkumākak tuṇṭupaṭṭuk kiṭappatu; kaṭaliṉ uḷvaḷivukaḷ neṭuntūram uḷnāṭṭilum  ceṉṟiruppa taṉāl, annāṭṭil eppakkamākac ceṉṟālum avvavaḷivu kaḷic cantikkavē nērum. 
alvāttīvu
innāṭṭiṉ mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkariyi aṭuttu oru tīvuk kūṭṭam uṇṭu. attīvukaḷil ulakiṉ mikap paḻam pāṟikaḷum, kaṭaliliruntu toṭaṅki malikaḷiṉ kīḻāka nilattiṉ aṭivayiṟṟik kuṭinta celvatu pōṉṟu nīṇṭu akaṉṟa kaṭaṟ kukikaḷum mikappala. annāṭṭu makkaḷ intīvuk kūṭṭatti hepriṭī°2 tīvukaḷ eṉpar. ittīvukaḷil oṉṟākiya alvā3 eṉpatiltāṉ ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ piṟantār.
mali nilamakkaḷ paṇpu
°kāṭlāntu malikaḷiṭiyē vāḻnta makkaḷ, ammaliyip pōlavē tiṇṇiya uṭalum karaṭu, muraṭāṉa vāḻkkiyum uṭiyavar. avarkaḷ oppaṟṟa vīrarkaḷ; cāviṟku añcāmal tam nāṭṭiṟkākap pōrāṭum peruntaki maṟavarkaḷ. āṉāl, amitik kālattil avarkaḷ kaḷḷam, kapaṭu aṟṟa uḻippāḷikaḷ; māṉa uṇarcciyuṭaṉ uṭal varunta uḻittu eḷiya vāḻkki vāḻpavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ viṭutali ārvamuṭiyavarkaḷ, tammi aṇṭiṉavari uyirtantum ātarikkum iyalpu uṭiyavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ mika uyar
vākap pēṇiya aṟam vāymi allatu uṇmi tavaṟāmiyēyākum. 
muṉṉōr vīram
ēṟakkuṟiyirunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ iṅkilānti
yum °kāṭlāntiyum ōr aracaṉ āṭci ceytāṉ. āṅkila nāṭṭu makkaḷ avaṉi veṟuttu nāṭṭiliruntu turatti viṭṭaṉar. vēṟu ōr araca parampariyiyum niṟuviṉar. °kāṭlāntu makkaḷ, ciṟappāka aṅkuḷḷa malināṭṭu makkaḷ, ipputiya parampariyi ēṟkātu paḻiya marapil vantavarkaḷiyē ātarittaṉar; avarkaḷiyē aracarākkak kiḷarcci ceytu pōrāṭiṉar. ittakiya perumpōrkaḷil ‘kallōṭaṉ’ eṉṟa iṭattil naṭipeṟṟa pōrum oṉṟākum. °kāṭlāntiluḷḷa malināṭṭu moḻiyākiya ‘keylik’ moḻiyil nāṭṭup pāṇar palar ippōrip paṟṟip pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ muppāṭṭaṉār oruvar ippōril īṭupaṭṭu uyir nīttu ‘nāṭṭu vīrar’eṉap pukaḻ peṟṟār. 
uyariya paṇpu
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ ciṟuvarāka irunta pōtu, tam pāṭṭaṉār mūlamākat tam muṉṉōrkaḷip paṟṟiya vīrak katikaḷ pala kēṭṭaṟintār. ataṉāl  appāṭṭaṉārukku muntiya āṟu talimuṟikaḷ variyuḷḷa muṉṉōr varalāṟu muḻuvatum liviṅ°ṭaṉukkut teriyavantatu. kuṭumpattiṉ paramparip paṇpukaḷuḷ, pāṭṭaṉār mika perumiyuṭaṉ aṭikkaṭi eṭuttukkūṟiya ceyti, ‘akkuṭumpattil evarum vāymi tavaṟiyatu kiṭiyātu’ eṉpatēyākum. ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉum tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum ippaṇpil ettakiya kuṟiyum varāmal kāttuvantār. 
pāṭṭaṉār
liviṅ°ṭaṉ kuṭumpattār 1792 ām āṇṭiliruntē kiḷā°kō4, nakarattil vanta kuṭiyēṟi iruntaṉar. aṅku liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭiya pāṭṭaṉār oru pañcāliyil vēli pārttu vantār. avaruṭiya vāymiyik kaṇṭa āli mutalāḷikaḷukku avar mītu paṟṟutalum nampikkiyum mikuntaṉa. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa peruntokikaḷi nitiliyaṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkavō, aṅkē koṇṭupōyk koṭukkavō nērntapōtellām avarkaḷ avar mūlamākavē attoki kaḷi nampikkiyākak koṭuttaṉuppiṉārkaḷ. 
peṟṟōr
pāṭṭaṉārukkup piḷḷikaḷ palar iruntaṉar. ṭēviṭiṉ tantiyākiya nīl liviṅ°ṭaṉit 5 tavira maṟṟavar ellōrum aracāṅka aluvalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar. avar oruvar maṭṭum tēyili vāṇikam ceytu vantaṉar. avar mikunta camayap paṟṟu uṭiyavar; ñāyiṟṟak kiḻamikaḷil kōvilukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭuvatuṭaṉ avvappōtu kōvil mēṭiyēṟic ciṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷum ceytuvantaṉar. 
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ tāyār ākṉi° 6 eṉpavar. avar ‘haṉṭar’7 eṉṟu kuṭippeyaruṭiya kuṭumpattic cārntavar. tāy tantiyar iruvarum kiḷā°kō nakarilēyē vīṭu amarttik koṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar. aṅkē 1813ām āṇṭu mārc mātam 18 ām nāḷil ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ piṟantār. 
ākṉi° liviṅ°ṭaṉ vīṭṭic cuttamākavum veḷiccam uṭiyatākavum vittukkoḷvatil tiṟami uṭiyavar. aṅkaṅkē aṭukkup palakikaḷilum mēṭip palakikaḷilum caṭṭaṅkaḷilum nallaṟivu tarakkūṭiya puttakaṅkaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum. ittakiya cuṟṟuc cārpukaḷiṭiyē piṟantu vaḷarnta ṭēviṭ, kalviyaṟivil iḷamimutalē nāṭṭaṅ koṇṭiruntami iyaṟkiyē aṉṟō?
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
	1.	ḷuuṭivaṭayaṉē  2. kūhna ḻanaceṉinaḷa, 3. kūhna aiḷaṭana ṭika ēieya 4. ḻuṭayaḷapaṭiṟa, 
5. cūnaṭi ṭuieipēḷavaṭinē, 6. yapanēḷa, 7. ḻaravēnaca
3. iḷami : kalviyil ārvam
iḷamic ceyalkaḷ
nīl liviṅ°ṭaṉukku aintu āṇ kuḻantikaḷum, iraṇṭu peṇ kuḻantikaḷum iruntaṉar. ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ avarkaḷil iraṇṭām piḷḷiyāvar. ivar iḷamiyilēyē tam tamiyaṉuṭaṉ cērntu vīṭṭuk kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉittut tāy tantiyarukku utavi ceyvār; vēliyillāmal ōynta nēraṅkaḷil ūrppuṟaṅkaḷilum kaṭaṟkariyilum  ceṉṟu ulāvuvār. appōtu āṅkuk kāṇum paṟpala vaṇṇap pūkkaḷiyum, palavakic cippikaḷiyum, kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷiyum tiraṭṭik koṇṭu varuvār, āṟukaḷil tūṇṭiliṭṭu mīṉ piṭittum, ōṭiyāṭi nīntiyum poḻutu pōkkuvār. 
uyariya eṇṇam
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ kuṭumpam mikka celvam uṭiyataṉṟu. ātalāl ivar, tam pattām vayatilēyē pāṭṭaṉār toḻilil īṭupaṭṭup pañcu āliyil vēli pārkkat toṭaṅkiṉār. āṉāl  iḷamiyilēyē iva
ruṭiya aṉṉiyār ivarukkup puttakaṅkaḷ mītu paṟṟutaliyum, camūka cēvi ceyyum eṇṇattiyum ūṭṭiyiruntār. ākavē, ṭēviṭ kūlivēli ceyyum
pōtum tammāl muṭinta aḷavu ariya nūlkaḷik kaṟpatil tam ōyvu nērattik kaḻittār. ivar, āliyil iyantiraṅ
kaḷukkiṭiyē, ētēṉum oru puttakatti virittu māṭṭi vittiruppār; vēlikkiṭiyil kiṭikkum ciṟitu ōyvu nēraṅkaḷilum avaṟṟi āvaluṭaṉ paṭittukoṇṭu iruppār. ivar, avvappōtu tammiviṭat tuṉpappaṭum ēḻikaḷukku utavi ceyvār; ulakiṉ tuṉpappaṭupavarkkeṉat tammāl etirkālattil ciṟitaḷavāyiṉum utavi ceyyak kūṭamā eṉṟu ārāyvār. avveṇṇam niṟivēṟutaṟkāka ivar eppōtum tiṭṭamiṭṭukkoṇṭē iruppār. 
kalviyil ārvam
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, tām peṟum campaḷattil oru pakutiyit tam maṉattukkup poruttamāṉa puttakaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvatilēyē celavu ceytār. tiṟampaṭṭa āciriyar kaṟpittālum māṇavarkku eḷitil viḷaṅkāt taṉmiyatu ilattīṉ1 ilakkaṇam liviṅ°ṭaṉ attakiya ilakkaṇattip piṟar utaviyiṉṟit tāmākak kaṟṟaṟintār. ic ceyal oṉṟē, ivaratu puttaka ārvam evvaḷavu eṉpati aḷaviṭṭu urikkumaṉṟō! ivar āliyil vēli ceytu kaḻitta āṟu āṇṭiṟkuḷ ilattīṉ moḻiyil naṉku tērcci peṟṟār. attērcciyiṉ utaviyāl virjil 2, hore° 3 mutaliyōr eḻutiya ciṟappuvāynta ilakkiya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟu, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷi aṟinta koḷḷattakka aṟivuṭiyavarāṉār. 
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, kāli āṟumaṇi mutal iravu eṭṭu
maṇivari āliyil vēli ceyvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl ivar, mēlum tam aṟivi vaḷarkka virumpiṉār. eṉavē ivar, āliyiliruntu vanta piṉṉum, āli mutalāḷikaḷ paṇa utavi ceytu naṭattivanta ēḻip piḷḷikaḷukkuriya paḷḷik kūṭam ceṉṟu oru maṇi nēram paṭittār. ataṉ piṉṉum uṟaṅka maṉamiṉṟip paṉṉiraṇṭu allatu oru maṇi vari tam vīṭṭil paṭittu vantār. iṅṅaṉam ivar ōyviṉṟip paṭittu varuvatit tāyār kaṇṭaṉar; iṅṅaṉam paṭippatāl taṉ makaṉiṉ uṭalnili keṭum eṉa avar añciṉār. ātalāl cila vēḷikaḷil avar, liviṅ°ṭaṉ kiyiluḷḷa puttakatti vāṅkip pūṭṭiya iṭattil vittu viṭṭu, ivari uṟaṅkac ceyvatum uṇṭu. 
aṟiviyal nūlkaḷ
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉ ikkālaṅkaḷil tamakkuk kiṭitta puttakaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum vācittār. āṉāl, puṉikatikaḷ (nāvalkaḷ) kiṭittāl avaṟṟik kaṇ
ṇeṭuttum pārār. itaṟkuk kāraṇam illāmalilli. ‘puṉikkatikaḷ, iṉpam maṭṭum tarumē aṉṟi vāḻk
kiyic cemmi ceyvataṟkāṉa paṭippiṉikaḷ tara
māṭṭā’ eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ niṉittār. ataṉāl ivar, puṉi katikaḷiviṭap pala nāṭukaḷilum cuṟṟit tirintavariṉ virivurikaḷi mikka āvaluṭaṉ paṭittār. aṟiviyal  nūlkaḷip paṭippatilum ivarukku viruppam mika uṇṭu. annāḷil camayattalivar palar, ‘aṟiviyal, camayattakku etirāṉatu’ eṉak karutiyiruntaṉar.  ikkāraṇattāl camayappaṟṟu mikka nīl liviṅ°ṭaṉ, taṉ makaṉ aṟiviyal nūlkaḷip paṭippatik kaṇṭittu vantār.  ataṉāl tam camayappaṟṟukku uṟuti ēṟṟappaṭṭatēyaṉṟik kuṟivu ēṟpaṭṭatilli eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ piṟkālaṅkaḷil kūṟiyatu uṇṭu. 
ārāycci poruḷkaḷ 
ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ piṟkāla vāḻkkiyil tōṉṟa irunta perumikku uriya aṟikuṟikaḷ, ivaruṭiya iḷamip paruvattilēyē ōraḷavu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivar, puttakaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟit tokukkat toṭaṅkiṉār; tamakku arumi eṉṟu tōṉṟiya poruḷkaḷic cērkkattoṭaṅkiṉār. avaṟṟip paṟṟik kuṟippukkuḷum eḻuti vantār. ivvēlikaḷ, ārāycciyāḷarkku mikap payaṉpaṭattakkaṉa. ivaratu ittakiya muyaṟci, camayap paṇiyiyum, maruttuvap paṇiyiyum oruṅkē āṟṟap pērutaviyāka iruntatu. ivaratu iḷamik kālattu  makkaṭpaṇi ārvamē, piṟkālattil taṉnalamaṟṟa uṇmic camayap paṇiyāka māṟiṟṟu. ‘camayap paṇi eṉpatu makkaṭ paṇiyē’ eṉpati ivar vāḻvu naṉṟāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭum. 
peyar poṟittal
liviṅ°ṭaṉ iḷamip paruvattil kāṇappaṭum kuṟi
pāṭukaḷi uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl, aviyum ivar uyarvukku utaviyaṉavē eṉpatu tōṉṟum ciṟupiḷḷikaḷ ūrukku arukil uḷḷa ‘pātvel’3 kōṭṭiyil ēṟuvatil oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar  pōṭṭiyiṭuvatuṇṭu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷiviṭa mika uyaramāṉa iṭattiṟkut tāvi ēṟuvār. aṅkē tam peyarip poṟittu mīḷvār. vāḻvil maṟṟa ellōriṉum uyarnta kuṟikkōḷ uṭiyavarāy uyarntu, ataṉil tam pukaḻ eḻappoṟitta piṟkāla vāḻkkikku, iḵtu oru nalla aṟikuṟi ākum. 
vīṭṭuvēli
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, taṉ tāyārukku utaviyāka vīṭṭu vēli
kaḷic ceyvatu vaḻakkam.  ivar vīṭṭip perukkuvatum uṇṭu. ivar attoḻilic ceyyap piṉvāṅkuvatilli. eṉiṉum, veḷiyār kāṇātavāṟu vāyiṟ katavi aṭitta piṉtāṉ ataṉic ceyya ivar oruppaṭavārām. piṟar avamatippip poruṭpaṭuttum ippaṇpu, ivariṭam piṉ nāḷil talikāṭṭavilli. aṟivukkum maṉaccāṉṟukkum otta kāriyaṅkaḷic ceyvatil piṟar avamatippi ivar tam piṟkālaṅkaḷil poruṭpaṭuttiyatilli. makkaḷ ava matippiyō pakimiyiyō poruṭpaṭutti yiruntāl iyēcu, mukamatu, puttar, irājārām mōkaṉ rāy pōṉṟavar ulakap periyārāka irukka māṭṭārkaḷallavā?
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	ṭuyavin
2.	ēcipaṭi
3.	ḻaṭicayanaḷa
4.	kṣaṭivāṟanaṭaṭa
4. uyar tarak kalviyum paṇiyēṟpum
maruttuvak kalvi
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, āliyil tam vēlikaḷi oḻuṅkuṟac ceytataṉ payaṉāka, ivaruṭiya 19 ām āṇṭil nūl nūṟpōrāka uyarvu peṟṟār. ipputiya vēli caṟṟē kaṭumiyāṉatāka iruntālum nalla ūtiyam taruvatāka iruntatu. ākavē, ivar tam ūtiyattil muṉṉilum mikutiyāka mikuttu vittuk kuḷirkāla viṭumuṟiyil kiḷā°kōp palkalik kaḻakattil naṭipeṟṟa maruttuvap payiṟci vakuppil cērntār. tām caṟantatoru makkaṭ paṇi āṟṟa  aḵtu utavum eṉṟa karuttuṭaṉēyē ivar, tam iḷamik kālap payiṟciyil maruttuvat tuṟiyiyum oru kūṟākat tērnteṭuttār. 
periyōr kūṭṭuṟavu
kiḷā°kō palkalik kaḻakam, ivar vēli ceytu
vanta iṭattiliruntu ēḻukal tolivil iruntatu. kuḷirkālattil ivvaḷavu tūram paṉikkaṭṭiyil naṭap
patik tavirkkum eṇṇattuṭaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam kuṭumpattiṉari aḻittuc ceṉṟu, annakarilēyē oru vīṭṭil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. ippalkalik kaḻakattila liviṅ°ṭaṉ, paṇṭi nākarika moḻiyāṉa kirēkka moḻippayiṟci peṟṟār. pēraṟiñar vārṭlā1 viṉ camayac coṟpoḻivukaḷik kēṭṭa liviṅ°ṭaṉ, attuṟiyil pērārvam koṇṭār. palkalik kaḻakattil aṟiviyal pērāciyarāka iruntavar. kirakām 2 eṉpavar avaruṭaṉ tuṇip pērāciriyarāka irunta jēm°yaṅ3 eṉparuṭaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻaki avarukku uṟṟa naṇparāṉār. laṇṭaṉ maruttuva maṉṉaravit talava4 varāṉa jē. ri°ṭaṉ peṉṉeṭ5 eṉpavarum ṭēviṭ liviṅ°ṭaṉukku aṟimukamāṉār. 
maruttuvattil tērcci
liviṅ°ṭaṉ kiḷā°kōvil iraṇṭām āṇṭup payiṟciyi mēṟkoṇṭapōtu, laṇṭaṉ camayap paṇi
yāḷar kaḻakattil cēra eṇṇaṅ koṇṭār. ivaratu viṇṇappam appoḻutikku ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. eṉavē ivar, jōsaḵp mūr eṉṟa naṇparuṭaṉ kaḻakat
tiṉarik kāṇac ceṉṟār. kaḻakattiṉar ivari, tiruttaki - riccarṭu sīsil6 eṉpavari aṭuttu utavippaṇi āṟṟumāṟu ēviṉar. mūṉṟu mātap paṇiyiṉ piṉ tērvil ivar ēṟkappaṭātu pōkavē, mīṇṭum mūṉṟu mātam utavip paṇi ceyya vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. kaḻakattār ivari mēṟku inti tīvukaḷukku aṉuppa ēṟpāṭu ceytiruntaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ avvāṇṭu naṭipeṟṟa  maruttuvat tērvil tēṟiṉār. 
āḵprikā cella ārvam
liviṅ°ṭaṉ maruttuvat tērvukkāka laṇṭaṉukku paṭikkac ceṉṟapōtu cēval7 eṉṟa mātiṉ vīṭṭil taṅkiṉār. avviṭattil mōpaṭ8 eṉṟa camayap paṉiyāḷarik kaṇṭār. avar nāṟpatu āṇṭukaṭku mēl āḵprikkāvil paṇiyāṟ ṟiyavar. avar, liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ naṭpup pūṇṭu, āḵprikkāvil tām āṟṟiya paṇi paṟṟiya varalāṟṟi ellām ivarukku viritturittār. itaṉ payaṉāka liviṅ°ṭaṉukkum, ‘āḵrikā ceṉṟu paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum’ eṉṟa avā uṇṭāyiṟṟu. avviruppattiṟkut taka camayak kaḻakam oṉṟu, ivari 1840 ām āṇṭu teṉ āḵprikkāviṟku aṉuppa muṉvantatu. 
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	ṉuṭiuvaṭica ṟuyacaṉaṭayaṟa
2.	ḻucayāyaa
3.	tuyaanaḷa luṭirapē
4.	ūhyaciayan ṭika vāna cuṭilayaṭa ānaṉiuyaṭa ḷuṭiunivala ṭika ṭuṭiṉēṭin
5.	tu.cuḷivaṭin kṣanannēvava 
6.	cune. cuiuhyacaṉa ūnuṭi
7.	ācaḷa. ḷunaṟanaṭaṭa
8.	āṭiṟayevava
5. teṉ āḵprikā cellal
camayap paṇikku arppaṇam
nam vāḻkkiyi uruvākkum ceyalkaḷ pala, nām eṇṇāmalēyē nam muṉ varuvatuṇṭu. āyiṉum nam uḷḷattic cārnta viruppu veṟuppukaḷ avaṟṟiṭiyē tamakku uriya tiṟatti nammiyum aṟiyāmalē tērnteṭuttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉa. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, tam kirāmattil uḷḷa oru camayappaṇi niliyattiṉ kiḷi mūlam  camayap paṇiyāḷar palaruṭiya varalāṟukaḷip paṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟār. avar kaḷukkākat tammāl iyaṉṟa aḷavu poruḷ utavi ceyyavum uṟuti ceytiruntār. āṉāl cār° kaṭ°lāḵpṭ1 eṉṟa cīṉap paṇiyāḷar, ‘camayattukkup paṉṉūṟu iḷiñar vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu vēṇṭukōḷ viṭuttār. ataṉi aṟinta liviṅ°ṭaṉ, poruḷ utavuvatuṭaṉ amiyāmal tam vāḻkki muḻuvatiyumē attuṟikkeṉa oppaṭikkat tuṇintār. 
liviṅ°ṭaṉ ciṟuvayatiliruntē ciṟucamaya uṭpirivu
kaḷip pārāṭṭātavar. ātaliṉ ivar, paranta aṭip
paṭiyum potu neṟippōkkum uṭiya laṇṭaṉ camayap paṇik kaḻakam cārntu toṇṭāṟṟa muṉ vantār. cīṉa nāṭṭukkuc cella eḻunta ivar eṇṇam, appōta aṅku naṭanta apiṉippōrāl2 caṟṟē taṭippaṭṭatu. appōtu liviṅ°ṭaṉ  mōpāṭṭiṉ āḵprikap paṇiyip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭār. ākavē ivar ārvattuṭaṉ ataṉi mēṟkoṇṭār. ēḻikaḷukku uḻippatē uyarvu  eṉakkoṇṭa ivar, nākarika ulakil ēḻmip paṭiyilirunta āḵprikāvil paṇiyāṟṟa muṉintatu poruttamuṭiyatē ākum. 
vāṉanūl kaṟṟal
liviṅ°ṭaṉ 1840 ām āṇṭu ṭicampar mātam 8 ām nāḷaṉṟu iṅkilāntiliruntu āḵprikā cellap puṟappaṭṭār. ivar payaṇam ceyta kappaliṉ peyar ‘jārj3’ eṉpatu. ivar payaṇattiṉpōtu aṟiñar ṭōṉālṭusaṉ4 eṉpavariṉ naṭpip peṟṟār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, avvaṟiñariṉ utaviyāl vāṉil uḷḷa kōḷkaḷik kāṇavum avaṟṟiṉ niliyi aḷaviṭṭaṟiyum karuviyi vaḻaṅkavum kaṟṟuk koṇṭār. piṟkālattil pālivaṉaṅkaḷil tici teriyātu cellavēṇṭivantapoḻutu liviṅ°ṭaṉukku avvaṟivu mikap payaṉuṭiyatāka iruntatu. 
potunalac coṟpoḻivu
payaṇattiṉ naṭuvil naṉṉampikki muṉi5 yil kappal oru tiṅkaḷ taṅkiviṭṭatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ aṅkē iṟaṅkic cilakālam kariyōrattiluḷḷa veḷḷi makkaḷuṭaṉum nāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaṉum paḻaka nērntatu. aṅkuḷḷa kuṭiyēṟṟa makkaḷiṉ camayat talivar aṟiñar ḵpilip6 eṉpavar āvar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ avaruṭaṉ kōvil ceṉṟu vaḻipāṭāṟṟiṉār; ‘kaṭavuḷiṉ karuṇi uḷḷam’ eṉpatip paṟṟip pēciṉār. makkaḷ aṉivariṭattum “naṉ makkaḷ orē vakiyāṉa aṉpu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. niṟam, nākarikam, nāṭu, kalvi ākiya evaṟṟālum makkaḷiṭiyē vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭak kūṭātu” nam makkaḷ orē vakiyāṉa aṉpu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. niṟam, nākarikam, nāṭu, kalvi ākiya evaṟṟālum makkaḷiṭiyē vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭak kūṭātu” eṉpatē liviṅ°ṭaṉ pēciyataṉ curukkamākum. oru cilar appēcci vāṉaḷāvap pukaḻntaṉar, vēṟucilar paḻittaṉarām. liviṅ°ṭaṉ vēṟupāṭu aṟiyāmal tikittaṉar. cilar āḵprik makkaḷi veḷḷiyaruṭaṉ oppākak karutāmal vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭi vantaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ‘aṉivariyum oṉṟupōla naṭattavēṇṭum’ eṉak kūṟiya potu uṇmi avarkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkip pōkkik kuttikkāṭṭuvatu
pōl iruntatu. ivar, ataṉip piṉṉarē aṟintār. 
kurumāṉil liviṅ°ṭaṉ
oru tiṅkaḷukkuppiṉ kappal naṉṉampiṉki muṉiyi
liruntu puṟappaṭṭatu, puṟappaṭṭa cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ alkō virikuṭā7 vantu cērntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ kappaliṉiṉṟu iṟaṅki, māṭṭu vaṇṭiyil ēṟic cila nāṭkaḷil kurumāṉ8 eṉṟa iṭam vantu cērntār. ituvē aṟiñar mōpaṭ, neṭunāḷ camayappaṇi  āṟṟiya iṭamākum. avar, liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam tam vāḻkkiyiṉ paṭippiṉikaḷi eṭatturikkiyil, ‘kaṭaṟkarippakutikaḷil camayappaṇiyāḷar palariṭiyē iruntu paṇi āṟṟuvatiṉum uḷ nāṭṭil piṟar cellāta ellivari ceṉṟu, puttam putiya makkaḷiṭiyē paṇi āṟṟuvatē nallatu’ eṉak kūṟiyiruntār. anta aṟivuriyiṉ uṇmiyi liviṅ°ṭaṉ virivil kaṇṭu koṇṭār. 
potunalat toṇṭu
liviṅ°ṭaṉukku muṉ ivviṭam vantu paṇiyāṟṟi
yavar palarum, camaya uṭpirivukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭavar. avar tamakkuḷ māṟupaṭṭa karuttiṉarāy, oruvari oruvar etirttut tam koḷkikaḷip parappiṉar. mēlum avarkaḷ camayap paṇiyuṭaṉē vāṇika, araciyal nalaṅkaḷiyum kalantu ceyalāṟṟiṉar. ātalāl avar, kariyōrak kuṭiyēṟṟa makkaḷiṉ naṉmatippip peṟātu, aṉṉāriṉ avanampikkiyiyē peṟṟaṉar. itaṉi liviṅ°ṭaṉ naṉku aṟintavar. ātalāl ivar, ‘nākarika vakiyil kuḻantip paruvattiṉarāṉa avar kaḷiṭiyē uyarnta muṟiyil uṟavāṭip putiya iṭattil putiya niliyi uṇṭu paṇṇavēṇṭum’ eṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ‘tūya aṉpi aṉpiṉālum, taṉnala maṟṟa uḻippiṉālum maṭṭumē peṟamuṭiyum’ eṉpatit tam nīṇṭa paṇi vāḻvil kāṭṭalāṉār’ liviṅ°ṭaṉ ikkaruttuṭaṉ, kurumāṉi viṭṭu uḷnāṭu cellum vakiyil tammip payiṟṟuvittuk koḷḷat toṭaṅkiṉār. 
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	ūhyacaṭanaḷaḷa ḻuravaṇaṭayamava
2.	ṭīyīria ṟayacaḷa in ūhiyē
3.	kūhna ḻunaṭicapana, 
4.	ṉuca. ṉuṭiyēṭaṉa ḷuṭin
5.	ūyayīna ṭika ḻuṭiṭiṉa ḻaṭiyīna
6.	ṉuca. ñāṭiki 
7.	kṣa. haṭapaṭiya kṣayala 
8. 	muracaraayan ḷuvayaviṭin 
6. kurumāṉ paṇiniliyam
uḷnāṭṭup pirayāṇam
kurumāṉ paṇiniliyam, alkō kaṭaṟkariyiliruntu 700 kal tūram uḷnāṭṭil vilaki iruntatu. attūram muḻuvatum liviṅ°ṭaṉ māṭṭu vaṇṭiyilēyē payaṇam ceytataṉāl, annāṭṭiyum, nāṭṭu makkaḷiyum kūrntu kavaṉikka vāyppup peṟṟār. nila iyal vakiyil annāṭu, liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ tāy nāṭākiya °kāṭlāntip pōlavē kalluṅkaraṭumāka mali aṭarntiruntatu. 
nāṭṭu makkaḷ
makkaḷum oruvakiyil °kāṭlāntu makkaḷiyē ottiruntaṉar. avar, cūtumvātum aṟṟavar, ayalavari aṉpuṭaṉ ēṟṟut tōḻami koṇṭāṭupavar. āṉāl, avarkaḷ maṉattil ciṟataḷavu aiyam ēṟpaṭṭālumpōtum, uṭaṉē avarkaḷiṉ uḷḷārnta muraṭṭuk kuṇamum koṭumiyum kiḷarntu veḷippaṭum. camaya vakiyil avar pala mūṭa nampikkikaḷi uṭiyavarāka iruntaṉar. mantira vātikaḷ, cūṉiyakkārarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷip paṟṟiya accam, avarkaḷukku mika uṇṭu. avarkaḷiṭiyē maruttuvarē mantirakkārarākavum iruntaṉar. ataṉōṭu avarkaḷē maḻiyic cilavakic caṭaṅkukaḷāl koṇṭu varum āṟṟal uṭiyavarkaḷākavum karutappaṭṭaṉar. ākavē,  liviṅ°ṭaṉ maruttuvappaṇi, avarkaḷiṭiyē celvākku aṭivataṟku neṭunāṭkaḷ āyiṉa.
nāṭṭumakkaḷum uṭpirivukaḷum
kurumāṉ variyuḷḷa pakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ pekkuvāṉar1 eṉappaṭuvar. avviṭam pekku vāṉānāṭu eṉappaṭum. avar ekiptiyarip pōlavē palavaki vilaṅkukaḷi vaṇaṅkupavarāka iruntaṉar. avarkaḷiṭiyē pala uṭpirivukaḷ iruntaṉa. oru piriviṉ peyar, appiriviṉar vaṇaṅkum vilaṅkiṉ peyari oṭṭi amintatākavē iruntata. pekuvaṉ (mutaliyiṉam) pakaṭlā (kuraṅkiṉam), paṭlāpi (mīṉiṉam) eṉpaṉa attakiya peyarkaḷuṭ cilavākum. ovvoru piriviṉarukkum ciṟappāṉa ovvoru kūttu uṇṭu. ataṉālum avarkaḷip pirittaṟiyakkūṭum. mēlum ovvoru piriviṉaram tām vaṇaṅkum teyvamākiya vilaṅkimaṭṭum koṉṟu uṇpatilli. 
nāṭṭu moḻippayiṟci
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, iṅkilāntiluḷḷa talimi niliyatti
liruntu varum uttaravi etirpārttuk koṇṭu mūṉṟu mātam vari kurumāṉil taṅkiyiruntār. uttaravu etuvum varavilli. tām cellum iṭam etuvāyiṉum  annāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ moḻi, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi aṟivatu iṉṟiyami yātatu eṉpati liviṅ°ṭaṉ aṟintavar. ataṉāl avar, airōppiyac cūḻniliyiliruntu maṟṟilum vilaki irunta liṭṭiparpā2 eṉṟa iṭattil ceṉṟu aṅkē payiṟci peṟalāṉār. airōppiyat toṭarpu illāmal payiṉṟa taṉ payaṉāka mikavirivilēyē annāṭṭu moḻiyilum, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷilum tērcci uṭiyavarāṉār. ataṉāl nāṭṭumakkaḷuḷ tāmum oruvar, eṉṟa nilimi yaṭintār. 
toṇṭiṟku iṭiñcal
liviṅ°ṭaṉukku muṉ āḵprikāvil ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉa veḷḷiyar palar, annāṭṭu makkaḷi mika iḻivāka naṭattivantaṉar. ataṉāl, iru cārāriṭiyum tappeṇṇamum pakimiyum aiyamaṉappāṉmiyum mikun
tiruntaṉa. ivi, toṭakkattil liviṅ°ṭaṉiyum tākkiṉa. eṉiṉum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ nērmi, peruntaṉmi mutaliya kuṇaṅkaḷ, palari ivarukku naṇparākkiṉa. liviṅ°ṭaṉ paṇiyik kaṇṭu poṟāmi uḷḷam paṭitta veḷḷiyar cilurum, nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē maruttuvarāka irunta cilarum ivarukku etirikaḷāyiṉar. avar, liviṅ°ṭaṉ ceyta paṇiyi veṟuttaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉip paṟṟiyum ivar ceyyum paṇiyip paṟṟiyum uḷnāṭṭil tavaṟāṉa karuttuk
kaḷip parappiṉar. ikkaruttukkaḷ vērūṉṟip paravuvataṉ muṉpē makkaḷiṭam nēraṭiyāṉa uṟavu ēṟpaṭuttik koḷḷa liviṅ°ṭaṉ virumpiṉār. ivar mutalil liṭṭiparpāvil kuṭiyēṟa ēṟpāṭu ceytiruntār; ikkaruttuṭaṉēyē kurumāṉukkum vantār. āṉāl aṅkuḷḷa pakkuvāṉar
kaḷip ‘pāralā°’ eṉṟa vakuppiṉar etirttut turatti
viṭṭaṉar. ic ceyti liviṅ°ṭaṉukku eṭṭiṟṟu. ataṉāl, vēṟu iṭam pārkkavēṇṭiya nili ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
aṉātip peṇ
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, vaṇṭi oṉṟiṉ tuṇikoṇṭu kurumāṉukkut tirumpiṉār. ivaruṭaṉ nāṭṭumakkaḷ cilarum payaṇam ceytaṉar. avar payaṇam ceyta vaṇṭi
yiṉ piṉ, patiṉoṉṟu, allatu paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatuṭiya peṇ orutti toṭarntāḷ. avaḷ taṉ tamakkiyiṉ ataravil vāḻntavaḷ. tamakkē iṟakkavē ‘avaḷ tuṇiyaṟṟavaḷāṉāḷ. vēṟu kuṭumpattār avaḷi aṭimiyāka viṟkavum muṭivu ceytaṉar. it tīya eṇṇatti aṟinta annaṅki, avarkaḷ piṭiyiṉiṉṟum tappa muyaṉṟāḷ, vaḻiyil vaṇṭiyik kāṇavē, vaṇṭiyiluḷḷōr utaviyip peṟalākum eṉṟa nampikiyālē piṉ toṭaravāḷāyiṉaḷ. āṉāl, avaḷuṭiya āṇṭāṉ (ejamāṉaṉ) piṉ toṭaravē, avaḷ veṟināy kippaṭṭa pūṉikkuṭṭi pōlak kataṟiṉāḷ. liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ nāṭṭumakkaḷ palar payaṇam ceytaṉaraṉṟō? liviṅ°ṭaṉ, avaruḷ oruvaṉ utaviyāl, appeṇ aṇintirunta pāci maṇi mutaliyavaṟṟi viṟṟuk koṭuttu, avaḷi mīṭṭār. 
nīr vacati amitta vaki
liviṅ°ṭaṉ putiya kuṭiyēṟṟattiṟkāṉa iṭam pārttu, aṅkē payiriṭuvataṟku vēṇṭum nīrvacatikaḷi ēṟpaṭutta eṇṇiṉār. aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ, vāṉam pārttu maḻi varuvippatoṉṟu tavira nīr vēṭkiyit tīrkka vēṟu vaki aṟiyātavar. eṉavē liviṅ°ṭaṉukku at
takiya nāṭṭumakkaḷiṭamiruntu utavi peṟuvatu aritāka iruntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ceyta iṉiya cūḻcci oṉṟu ivvakiyil perumpalaṉ tantatu. nāṭṭu maruttuvar, maruntu taruvatu maṭṭum aṉṟi, maḻiyiyum aḻikkum taṉmiyuṭiyavar eṉpati muṉṉē kaṇṭōm. avar
kaḷip pōlavē liviṅ°ṭaṉ “nāṉum maḻiyi aḻikkap pōkiṟēṉ” eṉṟār. uṭaṉē yāvarum ataṟkāṉa ceyviṉi (caṭaṅku)kaḷukku utava muṉ vantaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ nayamāka, “eṅkaḷ nāṭṭil nāṅkaḷ maḻi varuvikkac ceyyum ceyviṉi, kālvāy veṭṭuvatē” eṉṟār. maruttuvar itukēṭṭup puṉṉaki ceyta pōtilum yārum maṉam nōka villi. makkaḷum uṭaṉ niṉṟu uḻittaṉar. 
“uruvu kaṇṭu eḷḷāmi vēṇṭum”
nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē naṉmatippup peṟum vakiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ avarkaḷ moḻiyil peṟṟirunta payiṟci, ciṟanta payaṉuṭiyatāka iruntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ annāṭṭu moḻiyi naṉku aṟintavar. eṉpatu teriyāta cilar, ivaruṭiya veḷiṟiya oṭuṅkiya uṭalikkaṇṭu, “ivar valuvaṟṟavar, camayam vāyttapōtu ivari aṭakki viṭalām” eṉap pēcik koṇṭaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, avarkaḷ, taṉ tavaṟutalivat tāmē aṟiyum vaṇṇam neṭuntolivu tam muḻu āṟṟalik kāṭṭi, virintunaṭantār. ivar mītu vacipēciya makkaḷ ivarip piṉpaṟṟi naṭakka muṭiyāmal nāypōl iḷittaṉarām!
iṉattalivaratu naṭpip peṟutal
pekkuvāṉariṉ talivar cecil3 eṉpavar. avar mutalil liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ camayap paṇiyi vaṉmiyāka etirttār, āṉāl, piṟarāl kuṇam aṭiyāta palar liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ maruttuvappaṇi mūlam kuṇam aṭivatik kaṇṭu maṉam māṟi, ivar vayappaṭṭār. oru camayam ceciliṉ kuḻanti nōyvāyp paṭṭatu. ataṉāl avar, peruṅkavali uṟṟār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ mika eḷitāka avaṉik kuṇappaṭuttiṉār. piṉṉarē attalivar, liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ nilitta naṭpukkoṇṭār.
nalla upatēcam
pekkuvāṉar ‘kikkuk ki, uyirkku uyir’ eṉṟa oḻukka muṟiyil tiḷittavar. 
“iṉṉā ceytāri oṟuttal avarnāṇa
  naṉṉayam ceytuviṭal”
eṉṟu kūṟiya tiruvaḷḷuvar neṟiyi ottatu iyēcunātar neṟi. eṉavē, liviṅ°ṭaṉ kūṟiya iyēcu perumāṉ poṉṉuri kaḷik kēṭṭu avarellām oṉṟum tōṉṟātavarāyiṉar, kuḻantikaḷ pōl kēḷvikaḷ kēṭṭaṉar, māṟimāṟik kuḻappamum, viyappum, makiḻcciyum aṭintaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ cila nāḷukkuḷ avarkaḷ moḻiyil viviliya nūliṉ pakutikaḷi moḻi peyarttatuṭaṉ, vaṇakkamuṟiyil pāṭalkaḷum iyaṟṟalāṉār. 
1843 ām āṇṭu jūṉil ‘kurumāṉiliruntu uḷ nāṭṭukku ēkalām’ eṉṟa uttaravu camaya caṅkattiliruntu vantatu. ataṉik kaṇṭa liviṅ°ṭaṉ elliyilā makiḻcciyuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭār. 
kuṟippukaḷ
1.	kṣanuhrayayēca
2.	ṭavivinecaye	
3	ḷunuhnaṭana

7. arimā vēṭṭi
eccarikkik kaṭitam
liviṅ°ṭaṉ taṉ camayap paṇikku nilikkaḷamākat tērnta iṭam ‘mapōṭsā’1 eṉpatākum. ittērvupaṟṟi ivar tam naṇparkaḷukku eḻuti, avarkaḷiṉ aṟivurikaḷi etirnōkki iruntār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ naṉṉampikki muṉikkuvarumpōtu, aṟiñar ḵpilip eṉpavari aṅkē kaṇṭāraṉṟō? avarē āḵprikāvil camayak kaḻakattiṉ ceyalāḷarāka2 iruntavar. avar taṟceyalākak kurumāṉukku vantār. accamayam liviṅ°ṭaṉ iṭa ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭut tolitūram ceṉṟiruntār. aṟiñar ḵpilip, liviṅ°ṭaṉ tirumpuvataṟkuḷḷākap pōkavēṇṭiyavarāṉār. eṉavē avar, liviṅ°ṭaṉukku oru kuṟippu eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟār. “ekkāraṇattālum mapōṭsāvil kuṭiyēṟuvatu naṉṟaṉṟu. acceyal, erimaliyiṉ erivāyil vīṭu amittu vāḻvatu pōlām. ēṉeṉil ataṉi aṭuttu ‘mokōlolō’3 eṉṟa kuḻuviṉ talivaṉāṉa ‘mosilikāṭsē’4  eṉpavaṉ iruk
kiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ mikak koṭiyavaṉ, veḷḷiyar kurutiyi uṇṇak kāttiruppōṉ”  eṉpatē avar kuṟippil eḻutiya ceytiyākum. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tirumpiya kāliyil icceytiyik kaṇṭu aciṉārallar. piṟaku ucāvi, aḵtu avvaḷavu uṇmiyaṉṟu eṉakkaṇṭār. māpōṭsāvil pekkuvāṉar vakuppic cērnta pakaṭlākkaḷē5 vāḻn
taṉar. avar mokōlolōkkaḷip pōl koṭiyavar allar; mēlum avarkaḷ pekkuvāṉarkaḷilum ciṟitu vīraṅ kuṟintavarkaḷē yāvar eṉpati liviṅ°ṭaṉ piṉ nāṭkaḷil aṟiyalāṉār. 
tuṇivar iruvar
1843 ām āṇṭu āka°ṭu tiṅkaḷil liviṅ°ṭaṉ kurumāṉiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mapōṭsāvukkup payaṇam toṭaṅkiṉār. appōta ivaruṭaṉ vēṟu veḷḷiyar iruvar avviṭatti nāṭicceṉṟaṉar. avar, tiru piriṅkiḷ6 sartāma°°ṭīl7 eṉpavarāvar. avar iruvarum kēḷikkikaḷil pērum pukaḻum peṟṟavar;  nam nāṭākiya intiyāvil palanāḷiruntu ataṉi naṉku aṟintavar. avaruḷ °ṭīl, paṭittalivarāka iruntavar; piṟkālattil paṭi mutalvar āṉavar. avaruṭaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉ neruṅkiya naṭpu uṭiyavarāṉār. 
eḷiya vāḻkki
liviṅ°ṭaṉ āḵprikaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakiyataṉāl ivar paṇikku, iraṇṭu taṉippaṭṭa naṉmikaḷ ēṟpaṭ
ṭiruntaṉa. avaruṭiya moḻiyi āḵprika nāṭṭu makaṉ oruvaṉip pōlavē ivar pēcap payiṉṟatu oṉṟu , āḵprika nāṭṭu nilikku otta annāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷik kik koṇṭami maṟṟoṉṟu. ivaruṭaṉ vanta maṟṟa āṅkilēya naṇpar ellām āravāramākap pala kutirikaḷ, kūṭāraṅkaḷ, mūṭṭi muṭiccukkaḷ, uṇavup poruṭkuvikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi uṭaṉ koṇṭu vantaṉar. itaṉāl avarkaḷ payaṇattil aṭinta tolli peritākum. liviṅ°ṭaṉō eḷiya āṭiyum, oru kaṭṭi vaṇṭiyum uṭiyavarāy, vēṟu evvakic cumiyum illāmal kivīcic ceṉṟār. āḵprika makkaḷippōl ivarukkum nilap parappē paṭukkiyākavum vāṉamaṇṭalamē kūriyākavum kāṟṟē pōrviyākavum payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. ‘makkaḷukkup paṇi ceyvōr iṉpavāḻvu tēṭuvatu, taṉ paṇikkuk kālkaṭṭu kikkaṭṭukaḷit tēṭikkoḷvatē ākum’ eṉpati liviṅ°ṭaṉ uṇarvār. ātaliṉ ivar eḷiya vāḻkkiyiyē mēṟkoṇṭār. 
payaṇattiṭiyē irākkālaṅkaḷil aṅkaṅkē taṅki, maṟunāḷ celvatē liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ vaḻakkam. ivar eṅkeṅkut taṅkukiṉṟārō āṅkāṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ utavi koṇṭu vaṇṭi māṭukaḷi aviḻttu viṭuvār. marattaṭiyilum otukkiṭaṅkaḷilumē taṅkuvār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ an nāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaṉiruntu viṟaku cērttuc camittu uṇṭu uṟaṅkuvār, ammakkaḷuṭaṉ oruvarākak kalantu uṟavāṭuvār. itaṉāl avar ellām, vēlikkārar eṉṟa muṟiyil uḻikkāmal naṇpar eṉṟa muṟiyil ārvattuṭaṉ uḻittaṉar. 
ciṅkaṅkaḷiṉ tolli
liviṅ°ṭaṉ ivvaṇṇam makkaḷuṭiya aṉpiyum ātaraviyuṅkoṇṭu mapōṭsāvi aṭintār. aṅkum ivar, perumpālum piṟar utaviyiṉṟit tamakku oru ciṟu kuṭilik kaṭṭik koṇṭār. avar aṅkē mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittār. avar vāḻnta pakutiyic cuṟṟic ciṅkaṅkaḷiṉ tolli peritum uṇṭu. aṅku vāḻnta pakaṭlākkaḷiṉ āṭumāṭukaḷukkum payirkaḷukkum avaṟṟāl pēraḻivu ēṟpaṭṭatu. cila camayam makkaḷ uyirkkum cētam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ it tolliyi nīkkum muyaṟciyil muṉintār. 
āḵprikap pakutikaḷil naṉku ūṭāṭiya liviṅ°ṭaṉ, ciṅkaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷi naṉku uṇarntu koṇṭār. an nāṭṭuc ciṅkaṅkaḷ perumpālum kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākavē cellum. oru ciṅkam eṅkāvatu kollappaṭṭāl kūṭṭam muḻumiyumē avviṭam viṭṭu akaṉṟuviṭumām. eṉavē liviṅ°ṭaṉ, ‘caṟṟu āṇmiyuṭaṉ oṉṟit tākki aḻittāl pōtum, it tolliyiliruntu makkaḷi viṭuvikkalām’ eṉṟu kaṇṭār. āṉāl, maṟṟa pekkuvāṉar vakuppukaḷukkuḷḷa añcāmiyum vīramum pakaṭlākkaḷukku illi. avar aṭikkaṭi ciṅkakkūṭṭattic cūḻntu muṟṟuki ceytaṉar. eṉiṉum taṉimiyāyc ciṅkam etirpaṭṭapōtu ataṉi etirttut tākkit tayaṅki ataṉit tappaviṭṭu vantaṉar. avar liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ ciṅkakkūṭṭam oṉṟi oru kuṉṟil vaḷittu niṉṟaṉar, appōtu ciṅkaṅkaḷi vaḻakkampōl tappic cella viṭuttaṉar. ataṉik kaṇṭa liviṅ°ṭaṉ, tāmē taṉippaṭṭavakiyil oru ciṅkatti etirkka muyaṉṟār. 
arimāvēṭṭi
‘mapālvē’7 eṉpavar ōr āḵprikap paḷḷi āciriyar. avar liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ iruntār. liviṅ°ṭaṉum anta āciriyarum, vēṭṭiyiṟ payiṟciyuṭiya maṟṟum cilaruṭaṉ cērntu aṅkē tappi ōṭa muyalum ciṅkaṅkaḷit tākka vēṇṭum  eṉap puṟappaṭṭaṉar. appōtu oru pāṟiyiṉ mēl uḷḷa putar maṟivil oru periya ciṅkam maṟintiruppatik kaṇṭaṉar. mapālvē ataṉic cuṭṭār. āṉāl, tuppākkik kuṇṭu pāṟimītu paṭṭut teṟittatu. ciṅkam cīṟṟattuṭaṉ kuṇṭupaṭṭa iṭattil ōṅki aṟintatu. piṉ, vaḷittu niṉṟa variciyip piḷantu koṇṭu ōṭiṟṟu. pakaṭlākkaḷ vaḻakkampōl vaḻiviṭṭaṉarē aṉṟi, ataṉit taṭukkavō tākkavō muyalavilli. mīṇṭum oru muṟi iraṇṭu iraṇṭu ciṅkaṅkaḷ vantaṉa. āṉāl, pakkattil cūḻntu niṉṟa āṭkaḷ mītu paṭṭuviṭumē eṉṟa accattiṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉum naṇparum avaṟṟic cuṭavilli. 
muṭivil aṉivarum ūri nōkkit tirumpiṉar. avvaḻiyil  maṟṟoru ciṅkam pāṟi arukil putaril piṉpakkamākat teṉpaṭṭatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam kiyiliruntu iru tōṭṭākkaḷi nirappic cuṭṭār. “vēṭṭuttavaṟavilli; viḻuntatu ciṅkam” eṉṟa kuraluṭaṉ aṉivarum ciṅkatti aṇukiṉar. itaṟkiṭiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ cuṭṭa mūṉṟāvatu kuṇṭum ciṅkattit tuḷittatu. āyiṉum, ataṉ arukil avarkaḷ ceṉṟapōtu mūṉṟu kuṇṭukaḷi ēṟṟum atu cākāmal vekuḷiyuṭaṉ niṉṟatu. ataṉ tōṟṟam, pōr araṅkil veṟiyuṭaṉ niṟkum mallaṉ tōṟṟatti ottiruntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ veṟumiyāy viṭṭatum tuppākkiyil tōṭṭākkaḷi nirappiya vaṇṇam, “caṟṟup poṟuṅkaḷ, cuṭukiṟēṉ” eṉṟār. ataṟkuḷ ciṅkam vālic cuḻaṟṟik koṇṭu perumuḻakkattuṭaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉ mītu pāyntatu. ciṅkattiṉ valatu muṉkāl, liviṅ°ṭaṉ tōḷmītu taṉ muḻuvaṉmiyuṭaṉ ōṅki vīḻntatu. ciṅkamum liviṅ°ṭaṉum oruvari oruvar kaṭṭippiṭittu uruṇṭu uyirukkup pōrāṭiṉar. oru muṟi, ciṅkattiṉaṭi ivar piṭariyil vīḻntu ivari uṇarviḻakkac ceytatu. 
vēṭṭiyiṉ palaṉ
ēṟattāḻa muppatu muḻattolivil niṉṟirunta mapālvē iru muṟi ciṅkattic cuṭṭār. kuṟitavaṟiṟṟu. āṉāl ataṉ payaṉākac ciṅkam taṉ piṭiyuḷ irunta liviṅ°ṭaṉi viṭṭuviṭṭu mapālvēmītu pāyntatu. muṉ orukāl liviṅ°ṭaṉāl uyirkākkap peṟṟa vīraṉ oruvaṉ iccamayam īṭṭiyāl ciṅkattiṉ vilāvil tākkiṉāṉ. ciṅkam mapālvēyiyum viṭṭu avaṉ mītu pāyalāyiṟṟu. āṉāl itaṟkuḷ ciṅkattiṉ vallami kuṉṟiviṭṭatu. muṉpu kuṇṭukaḷ tuḷitta kāyattiliruntu perukiya kurutiyālum, taṉ tākkutal vēkattālum atu cōrvaṭintu vīḻntu iṟantatu. ic ciṅkam iṟantu viḻuntatik kaṇṭa piṟaku ciṅkaṅkaḷ appakkam nāṭātu akaṉṟaṉa. ic ciṅka vēṭṭiyil yārum uyir iḻakkavilli. āyiṉum ciṅkattiṉ valiya nakaṅkaḷālum naccuppaṟkaḷālum iruvar paṭukāyam aṭintaṉar. avaruḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉum oruvar. ivaruṭiya tōḷpaṭṭi elumpukaḷ cukku nūṟāyiṉa. ciṅkattiṉ paṟkaḷ uṭuruviyatāl aṭikkaṭi vali ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉum, ivar vēṭṭikkāka aṇintirunta tōl kacci, paṟkaḷiṉ nañcupaṭāmal kāttatu. eṉavē, liviṅ°ṭaṉ pūṭṭaṟṟut toṅkiya kiyuṭaṉ tappiṉār. maṟṟavarō attakiya pātukāppu aṟṟu iruntataṉāl āṇṭu tavaṟāmal naccuk kāyccalukkum naccuvalikkum āḷākit tuṉpuṟṟaṉar.

8. maṇa vāḻkki
kātal maṇam
1844- ām āṇṭil aṟiñar mōpaṭ maṟupaṭiyum kurumāṉukku vantu taṅkiṉār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ avariyē ñāṉa kuruvākak koṇṭiruntār. ātalāl ivar, tam kuruviṉ kuṭilukku aṭikkaṭi celvār. accamayam mōpaṭṭiṉ mūtta putalviyarāṉa mēriyi liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṇṭu paḻaka nērntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ vīrappukaḻ mēriyiṉ maṉattik kavarntatil viyappilli, avvammiyār, ‘āṅkilēyap peṇmaṇi evarukkum attakiya vīravāḻvil paṅkukoḷvativiṭa uyarnta pēṟu vēṟu illi’ eṉa niṉittār. liviṅ°ṭaṉum, ammiyāriṉ aṭakkam, kuṇam, aṟivup payiṟci ākiyavaṟṟi nōkki, avariṭam māṟāppaṟṟu koṇṭār. orunāḷ māli, mēritam kiyāl nīr vārttuk kātta ciṟu pūñcōliyil iruvarum ulavac ceṉṟaṉar. appōtu avaravaruṭiya maṉattil taṉittaṉi ulaviya eṇṇaṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ uriyāṭal mūlam oṉṟupaṭṭuk kalantaṉa. aṟiñar mōpaṭṭum avaruṭiya kātali iṉitu varavēṟṟataṉāl, avar virivil maṇavāḻkki eṉṉum tēril pūṭṭap
peṟṟaṉar. 
nalvāḻvu
vāḻkkit tuṇivar iruvarum mapōṭsāvil ōrāṇṭu taṅkiṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ituvari veṟuñ ceṅkal koṇṭu kaṭṭiya maṉakkōṭṭikaḷ tiruvāṭṭi liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ mūlam cuṇṇāmpum kāriyum kalantu kaṭṭiya uṟutiyuṭiya kōṭṭikaḷ āyiṉa. mōpaṭ palanāḷ āḵprikāvil vāḻntu,annāṭṭil paḻakiyavar. accamayap paṇiyāḷariṉ celviyāratu ottuḻippiṉāl, liviṅ°ṭaṉ vāḻvu, tuṉpattiṭiyum iṉpam peṟṟu, niṟivum naṟpayaṉum uṭiyatāyiṟṟu. 
9. seccī° maṉamāṟṟam
iṭam māṟutal
liviṅ°ṭaṉ iṉavēṟupāṭu, niṟavēṟupāṭu, vakuppu vēṟupāṭu ākiya ellā vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum kaṭanta paranta aruḷ uḷḷam paṭittavar. ātalāl ivaratu camayappaṇi, piṟa paṇiyāḷar paṇiyippōl āḷtiraṭṭi āṭci vaṉmi kōyil paṇiyāka amiyavilli. ivar iyēcu perumāṉiṉ pērāl veḷḷiyar uyarvi nilināṭṭa muṉintiruntāl, nāṭṭu vēṟṟumi, moḻi vēṟṟumi ākiyavaṟṟiyum poruṭpaṭuttāmal ṭaccukkārar, jermaṉiyar mutaliya palvēṟu vakiyiṉarāṉa piṟa paṇiyāḷar ivari ōraḷavu ātarittiruppar. ituvaṉṟi ivar, āṅkila uyarvukkākap pāṭupaṭṭiruppiṉum ivari ōraḷavē avar etirttiruppār. āṉāl ivar enta maṉita vakuppiṉ āṭciyiyum uṟutippaṭutta virumpiṉār allar; uṇmiyākak kaṭavuḷ āṭciyi, ulaka opparavu āṭciyi nili nāṭṭavē muyaṉṟār. icceyalē piṟa paṇiyāḷarkkellām vayiṟṟu nōvi uṇṭākkiṟṟu. eṉavē, camayap paṇik kaḻakattiṉar palar liviṅ°ṭaṉōṭu muraṇpaṭṭaṉar. ātalāl, liviṅ°ṭaṉ, avarkaḷ tollikaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭum eṇṇattuṭaṉ, mēlum uḷnāṭu nōkkip payaṇappaṭṭār; mapōṭsāvi liruntu puṟappaṭṭu ataṟku nāṟpatu kal vaṭakkiluḷḷa “cōṉuvēṉ”1 eṉṉum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟār.
palavēlikaḷ
avar aṅkum mapōṭsāvip pōlavē vāḻk
kikku uriya ellā vacatikaḷiyum toṭakkattiliruntē ceyyavēṇṭiyavarāṉār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ aṅkut tam vāḻkkiyip paṟṟit tam kikkuṟippēṭṭil varin
tuḷḷa ceyti, cuvi taruvatākum. “kaṭṭaṭa vēli, tōṭṭa
vēli, cerupput tittal, maruttuvam, kaṉṉāṉvēli, taccu tuppākki tiruttutal, kolluli vaḷarttal, vaṇṭi ceppaṉiṭutal, camayac coṟpoḻivu, paḷḷip pōtaṉi, aṟivu nūl pukaṭṭutal mutaliyaṉavum māṇavarāṉa iraṇṭu mūṉṟu pērkkup pēruri āṟṟutalum eṉ nērattip paṅku koḷkiṉṟaṉa. eṉ vāḻkkit tuṇivi atē camayattil meḻuku varttikaḷ ceytal, cavukkāram ceytal, āṭi neytal mutaliyavaṟṟil īṭupaṭṭiruppāḷ. ivvakiyil āḵprikac camayap paṇikkāṉa oru kuṭumpam eṉṟa niliyi nāṅkaḷ vaṇmiyuṭaṉ aṟṟiṉōm, aḵtāvatu, veḷivēli kaḷil katampa vēliyāḷākak kaṇavaṉum, vīṭṭu vēlikaḷil aviyal paṇiyāḷāy maṉiviyum amintōm”
seccīl matamāṟṟam
cōṉuvēṉil vāḻnta pekkuvāṉarkaḷiṉ talivar seccīl eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiyuḷḷōm allavā? avarē liviṅ°ṭaṉ camaya pōtaṉiyi ēṟṟa mutal tōḻar āvar. iyēcunātariṉ nallurikaḷukkuc cevitanta cila nāṭ
kaḷukkuḷḷākavē avar, liviṅ°ṭaṉ moḻi peyart
tirunta viviliya nūlik kaṟkavum toṭaṅkiṉār. avaratu kuḻanti uḷḷam putumi ārvattāl putiya camayatti āttirattuṭaṉ viḻuṅka muyaṉṟatu. eṉiṉum ataṉic cerittuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu atu tikittatu. paḻami vācaṉi, etirpārāt taṭikaḷi uṇṭākkiṟṟu. cūḻniliyālum pala iṭiyūṟukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉatu, kiṟi°tu neṟiyi ēṟṟataṉāl avar tām muṉ maṇantirunta paṉ maṉiviyaruḷ orutti nīṅkalāka ēṉiyōri akaṟṟavēṇṭi nērntatēyām. avaruḷ palarum pekukvāṉariṭiyē mutaṟkuṭiyil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷātalāl, seccīl akkuṭikaḷiṉ uḷḷārnta veṟuppukku āḷāṉār. 
kāṭṭu makkaḷ tuṉpanili
liviṅ°ṭaṉ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ appakutiyil karuppu tōṉṟi nīṇṭanāḷ maḻi peyyātiruntatu. ‘ikkaruppu, makkaḷ paḻiya neṟiyi viṭṭu liviṅ°ṭaṉ vaṇakka muṟiyi ēṟṟataṉāltāṉ ēṟpaṭṭatu’ eṉap pekkuvāṉar eṇṇiṉar. maḻiyillāmal nāṭu vaṟaṇṭa kālaṅkaḷil talivaṉ maḻiyic ceyviṉi kaḷāl varuvippatu annāṭṭu vaḻakkam. seccīlō, putiya camayakkaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉāl ataṉic ceyya muṭiyāta varāṉār. makkaḷ aṉivarum liviṅ°ṭaṉ kālil viḻuntu oru muṟiyēṉum seccīlit tam naccuk kaṭṭuppāṭṭiliruntu viṭuvittut tamakku uyir taruvikkumpaṭi kaṇṇīrum kampaliyumākak keñciṉar. eṉ ceyvār liviṅ°ṭaṉ! ivar camaya uṟutikku, itu pōṉṟa paritāpamāṉa cōtaṉi eṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭatilli.
kalvi aḷittal
ivvaḷavu nerukkaṭiyilum liviṅ°ṭaṉ maṉa
muṭiyāmal tam paṇiyit taḷarātu vaḷarttē vantār. nāṭṭu makkaḷ palari āciriyarākap payiṟṟuvittār. avarkaḷik koṇṭē nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē kalviyip parappiṉār. ituvē, liviṅ°ṭaṉ ivviṭattil āṟṟiya toṇṭakaḷil tali ciṟantatu. 
putiya tolli
liviṅ°ṭaṉukku maṟṟoru tuṟiyil ēṟpaṭṭa tuyaram, ittuyarkaḷi yellām maṟakkac ceyyatatāka iruntatu. ṭaccukkārarāṉa pūyarkaḷ, liviṅ°ṭaṉ kuṭiyēṟiya pakutimutal kīḻk kaṭaṟkarivari paravi iruntaṉar. avar potuvāka āṅkilēyaruṭiya muṟikaḷiyum liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭiya muṟikaḷiyum veṟuttaṉar. ēṉeṉil avaṟṟāl nāṭṭumakkaḷ aṟivum  oṟṟumiyum vaṉmiyum peṟṟu, veḷḷiyarkku aṭimiyāka vāḻvatil veṟuppuṟuvar eṉṟu avar eṇṇiṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ paṇi mutircci peṟumuṉ avar maṉṉēṟit tākkip pekkuvāṉaric cūṟiyāṭat tuṭittu niṉṟaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, pīyar talivarāṉa kurukarik2 kaṇṭu nilimiyic ciṟitaḷavu taḷarttiṉar. eṉiṉum mēṉmēlum vaḷarntu varum poṟāmip puyalukku ōyvu kāṇa muṭiyavilli. 
iṭam māṟa muyaṟci
ivviṭariṉiṉṟum tappuvatu eṅṅaṉam eṉa liviṅ°
ṭaṉum naṇparum ārāyntaṉar. teṟkilum kiḻakkilum pōka vaḻiyilli; aṅkē pūyarkaḷum  piṟa veḷḷiyarkaḷum nilipeṟṟiruntaṉar. vaṭakkilum mēṟkilumō, iyaṟki aṉṉiyiṉ cīṟṟamē uruveṭuttāṟ pōṉṟa kalahārip pālivaṉam3 kaṭaliṉam parakkak kiṭantatu. inniliyil yātu ceyvatu? kalahāri kaṭantāl ōr akaṉṟa nalla ēriyum, ataṉic cūḻa nalla iḷam pālinilamum uṇṭu eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ piṟar collak kēṭṭaṉar. eṉavē ivar aṅkuk kuṭiyēṟi vāḻa vāyppu uṇṭō eṉpati ārāyap puṟappaṭṭār. 
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	ūhṭirēyanē
2.	mucarapanaca
3.	muyaṭayāyaci ṭunaḷanacava		
10. kalahārip pālivaṉam
kalahārip pālivaṉam
ulakattil uḷḷa pālivaṉaṅkaḷuḷ parappilum cūṭṭilum mutaṉmi uṭiyavi vaṭa āḵprikāvil uḷḷa sahārāvum, mēli āciyāviluḷḷa arēpiyāvum, vaṭamēṟku intiyāviluḷḷa tār pālivaṉamumē ākum. ivaṟṟiṉum vemmiyil kuṟinta pālivaṉamum 
uṇṭu. teṉṉintiyāvil tirunelvēli vaṭṭattil tiruccentūri aṭuttu ‘kutiri muṭittēri’ eṉṉum pālivaṉam oṉṟuṇṭu. ituvē, tamiḻnāṭaṭṭār kāṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa ciṟu pālivaṉamākum. aḷavil itaṉiṉum periyatāyiṉum itupōlavē putarkaḷ āṅkāṅku niṟintatum āṅkāṅku vaṟṟivarum vaṟaṇṭa ūṟṟukkaḷ uṭiyatum ākiya oru pālivaṉamē kalahāri eṉpatu. ita teṉṉāḵprikāviṉ uṭ pakutiyil uḷḷatu. itaṉ vaṭa kōṭiyil ‘ṅāmi’1 eṉṟa ēri irukkiṟatu. ataṉi aṭuttuc ceḻippuḷḷa iṭam irukkiṟatu. avviṭattil pala āṟukaḷ pāykiṉṟaṉa. 
muṉṉōr muyaṟci
muṉṉē itaṉi nāṭic cila veḷḷiyar cella muyaṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum avar ammuyaṟciyil māṇṭu aḻintaṉar. ātaliṉ avar appālivaṉattippaṟṟi aṟinta aṟivum ulakiṟkup payaṉpaṭātu, avaruṭaṉ aḻintupōyiṟṟu. vēli veṟṟikaramāka ataṉik kaṭantu ‘ṅāmi’ yik kaṇṇuṟṟa mutal veḷḷiyar liviṅ°ṭaṉē āvar. 
sepiṭṭuvēṉ aruṅkuṇaṅkaḷ
liviṅ°ṭaṉ ikkāriyatti mummuṟi muyaṉṟu, pala muṟi tōṟṟa piṟakē veṟṟi kāṇa muṭintatu. mutalil ittuṟiyil  kavaṉam celuttak kāraṇamāka iruntavar sekcīlē āvar. kalahārip pālivaṉattukku appāl vāḻnta ‘makōlolō’2 vakuppiṉariṉ māperun taliva
ṉāṉa sepiṭṭuvēṉ3 eṉpavaṉ oru muṟi seccīliṉ uyirik kāttavaṉ. maṟṟum avarukkut talimippataviyum tantu ātarittavaṉ. mēlum, pūyarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭa pala vakuppiṉariṉ citaṟiya pakutikaḷit taṉṉuṭaṉ cērtta ātaritta aruḷāḷaṉum avaṉē, piṟa vakuppiṉari maṭṭum aṉṟip piṟanāṭṭiṉariyum varavēṟṟu atarikkum aruḷ uḷḷam koṇṭavaṉ avaṉ eṉpati seccīl kūṟiṉār. atukēṭṭa liviṅ°ṭaṉ avaṉik kāṇa avāviṉār. iḵtu iyaṟkiyē aṉṟō?
ā°vel naṭpu
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, immuṟi tam āruyir naṇparum āḵprika nāṭṭil uḷḷa veḷḷiyariṭiyē ‘oppuyar
vaṟṟa vēṭṭiyāḷar’ eṉṟu pukaḻpeṟṟavarumāṉa ā°vel4 eṉpavari uṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟār. liviṅ°ṭaṉukku ippoḻutu mūṉṟu piḷḷikaḷ irun
taṉar. mūṉṟām piḷḷikku ñāṉat tantiyāka iruntavarum . inta ā°velē yāvar. 
pālivaṉa yāttiri -1
pālivaṉattiṭiyē kiṭittaṟkariya arum poruḷ taṇṇīrē ākum. nīriṉṟi makkaḷum vilaṅkukaḷum aṭiyum tuyaram colloṇātatu. kāyccalāl varum tīmi maṟṟorupuṟam. āṟukaḷil ciṟitaḷavākak kiṭakkum nīrip
paruka eṇṇi iṟaṅkiṉālō, mutali emaṉkaḷ oḷintu kiṭakkum. ivvaṉattil mikak kuḷḷamāṉa ‘putar makkaḷ’5 eṉṟa oru vakuppiṉar uṇṭu. avarkaḷ kūriya matiyum puṟṟeṟumpukaḷ pōṉṟu cuṟucuṟuppum viṭāmuyaṟciyum uṭiyavarkaḷ; tīṅkaṟṟa uḷḷamum tiṟamum paṭittavarkaḷ. liviṅ°ṭaṉukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka vantavaṉ ivviṉattavaṉē. avaṉ, avvappōtu avarkaḷi viṭṭakaṉṟu tikikka vittum vantāṉ.  āyiṉum  cūtaṟṟa uḷḷattuṭaṉum kavarccimikka naṭippuṭaṉum taṟperumi tōṉṟa avaṉ, avarkaḷ muṉ naṭantu ceṉṟu āravāram ceyvāṉ. eṉavē avaṉi yārum ciṉantu koḷvatilli. aṅkaṅkē putarmakkaḷiṉ mātar avarkaḷik kaṇṭu ōṭuvar. piṟaku avarkaḷ taṇṇīr maṭṭumē kēṭpatu kaṇṭu vēḷāṇmiyuṭaṉ naṉku upacarippar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tanta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ, paricukaḷ akiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu avar makiḻntu kūttāṭiṉar. 
utavi maṟuppu
ciṟitu tūram ceṉṟatum “itō ‘ṅāmi’ ēri” eṉa ā°vel tuḷḷik kūttāṭiṉār. āṉāl, aḵtu uṇmi
yil nīraṉṟu, kāṉal oḷiyē eṉṟu kaṇṭu, avar ēmāṟṟam aṭintaṉar. neṭunāṭ ceṉṟapiṉ ‘jukā’5 eṉṟa āṟṟiyum, ataṉpiṉ ataṉiṉum peritāṉa ‘tamaṉākkiḷ’6 eṉṟa āṟṟiyum kaṇṭār. tamaṉākkiḷ kaṭantāl sepiṭṭu
vēṉic cantikkalām. āṉāl, anta āṟu akalamum āḻamum  uṭiyatāka iruntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ikkariyil iranta talivaṉiṭam ōṭam vēṇṭiṉar. sepiṭṭuvēṉiṭam poṟāmi koṇṭa attalivaṉ utava maṟuttaṉaṉ. eṉavē liviṅ°ṭaṉ, vēṟu vakiyiṉṟip puṟappaṭṭa iṭattiṟkē mīḷa vēṇṭi yiruntatu. ā°vel tām naṉṉampikki muṉi celvatāka vum aṅkiruntu nalla paṭaku aṉuppuvatākavum kūṟic ceṉṟār. 
pālivaṉa yāttiri-2
liviṅ°ṭaṉ vāḻkki naṭattiya iṭattil pañcam toṭarnta nili peṟṟatu, eṉavē ivar paḷḷi, payiṟcik
kūṭam yāvum cīrkulintaṉa, vēṟu vēliyiṉmiyāl 1850 ām āṇṭil iraṇṭāvatu muṟiyum pālivaṉattik kaṭakkum muyaṟciyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ īṭupaṭṭār. immuṟi kāyccalāl varuntiya aṟupatu veḷḷiyarik kākkumporuṭṭu, liviṅ°ṭaṉ avarkaḷukku nēr vaḻiviṭṭu vilakic cilanāḷ pōkkiṉar. itaṉiṭiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ piḷḷikaḷum vēliyāṭkaḷum kāyccalāl pīṭikkappaṭavē, immuṟiyum payaṉpeṟātu tirumpiṉar. ittaṟuvāyil tiruvāṭṭi liviṅ°ṭaṉ vaḻiyil karuvuyirttu oru peṇ makavu īṉṟār. āṟu tiṉaṅkaḷukkuḷ ammakavum nōyvāyppaṭṭu iṟantatu. ataṉ piṉ uṭal nalatti eṇṇi aṉivarum kurumāṉ vantaṉar. aṅkiruntu ā°velik kāṇac ceṉṟa avarkaḷ, avari etirē kaṇṭu mīṇṭārkaḷ. 
sepiṭṭuvēṉ aṉpu kāṭṭal
sepiṭṭuvēṉ, taṉṉi liviṅ°ṭaṉ kāṇa vantatu kēṭṭu, avarukku aḻippum paricilkaḷum tūtar mūlam aṉuppi yiruntāṉ. seccīl caṟṟum muṉ yōcaṉi illāmal, liviṅ°ṭaṉik kēḷāmalē tūtari aṉuppi
viṭṭār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ attūtaruṭaṉ ceṉṟiruntāl evvaḷavō tuṉpam kuṟintirukkum.
pālivaṉa yāttiri-3
liviṅ°ṭaṉ mūṉṟām muṟiyāka 1851 ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷil sepiṭṭuvēṉi nāṭicceṉṟār.  immuṟi avarkaḷ ‘tamuṉākkiḷ’ eṉṟa āṟṟi eḷitil kaṭantu, sepiṭṭuvēṉuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāviṉar. iruṇṭa āḵprika nāṭṭu vāḻviṉiṭiyē liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṇkaḷukku sepiṭṭuvēṉ oru pēroḷi viḷakkamākavē tikaḻntāṉ. avaṉ taṉ vāḻnāṭ kaḷukkuḷ pala vakuppiṉarukkum niḻal aḷittup pakiyaccam pōkkiya cāṉṟōṉ. avaṉ ciṟanta pōr vīraṉ, irappavarkku īttu uvakkum taṇṇaḷiyāṉ. avaṉ īki, aruḷ, naṭpu ākiya uyar kuṇaṅkaḷik koṭumuṭikaḷākak koṇṭa aruṭ kuṇakkuṉṟāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. 
payaṉpeṟāta muyaṟci
sepiṭṭuvēṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉi naṉku varavēṟṟāṉ; kuṭiyēṟa evviṭam vēṇṭiṉum avviṭam taruvatāka vākkaḷittāṉ. āyiṉum avaṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉum ivar paṇiyum muḻuppayaṉ peṟamuṭiyātu pōyiṟṟu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ vanta cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ avaṉi īral kācanōy piṭittatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, āḵprika nāṭṭiṉ karumāṇikkamāṉa avaṉit tam maruttuvattāl kuṇappaṭutta evvaḷavō āval uṭiyavarāka iruntār. āṉāl mūṭanampikki koṇṭaṉar. iyalpākavē avaṉukkut tītu nēriṭiṉum atu tammāl viḷintatu eṉakkoṇṭu annāṭṭu makkaḷ tamakkut tīṅku ceytavar eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ añcit  tam āvali aṭakkik koṇṭār. sepiṭṭuvēṉum virivil iyaṟki eytiṉāṉ. āṭcikku urimiyuṭiyavaḷāy vanta avaṉ putalvi ‘memōcciṣāṉē’yum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam nal eṇṇam uṭiyavaḷākavē iruntāḷ. āṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ kuṭiyiruppukkēṟṟa iṭam kāṇātu mīṇṭaṉar. 
kuṭumpattit tāynāṭṭukku aṉupputal
itaṟkiṭiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉum ā°velum kiḻakkil ‘ḵjāmpesi’7 āṟṟut talinilam variyil ceṉṟu vēvu pārttaṉar. iṟutiyil tiruvāṭṭi liviṅ°ṭaṉ uṭal nalattiyum, kuhntikaḷiṉ kalviyiyum eṇṇi, liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam kuṭumpatti iṅkilāntukkē aṉuppa uṟutikoṇṭār. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ naṉṉampikki maṉikkup payaṇamāṉārkaḷ.
uḻippip pārāṭṭutal
liviṅ°ṭaṉ ippayaṇaṅkaḷālum  avvappōtu nēriṭṭa iṉṉalkaḷālum peritum kaṭaṉpaṭṭu uḻaṉṟār. camayappaṇi niliyamō liviṅ°ṭaṉcelavukaḷi vīṇ
celavukaḷ eṉakkūṟi, avar vīrac ceyalkaḷi acaṭṭi
ceytatu. inniliyil oppaṟṟa taṉ matippuṭaṉ ā°vel, tam paṇṇi varumpaṭiyil  oru pakutiyi liviṅ°ṭaṉukku naṭpu urimiyuṭaṉ utaviṉar. attuṭaṉ aracāṅkamum, nila iyal maṉṉaravi ārāyccik kaḻakattārum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ paṇiyip pōṟṟa maṉvantu ‘ṅāmi’ ēriyip putuvatākak kaṇṭataṟkāka ivarukku 25 perum poṟkācukaḷ (kiṉikaḷ) vaḻaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉamākac camayattuṟiyilum makkaṭ paṇittuṟiyilum liviṅ°ṭaṉukku uriya pukaḻ avari vantu aṭiyum muṉṉarē, aṟiviyal ārāyccittuṟiyil pukaḻccelvi avari aṭiyalāṉāḷ. 
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	ṭuyamana cūpayai
2.	āyamaṭiṭaṭiṭaṭi
3.	ḷunabivāyanē
4.	ṭīḷanaṟanaṭaṭa
5.	cunaciya turapaya
6.	kūyaaran ūṭana
7.	cuienaca ṣyaanui
11. iraṇṭām pirayāṇam
tuṉpaṅkaḷukku iṭiyil toṇṭu
liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ iraṇṭām pirayāṇamē teṉ āḵprikāviṉ uḷnāṭṭup pakutiyik kīḻkkaṭalaṭaṉum mēlkaṭaluṭaṉum (intumā kaṭaṟkariyuṭaṉum aṭlāṇṭik mākaṭaṟkariyuṭaṉum) iṇittatu. itaṉāl annāṭu ulakappōkku varavu neṟikaḷuṭaṉum, nākarikap pōk
kuṭaṉum toṭarpu peṟṟatu. ayiṉum ipperuṅ kāriyattit toṭaṅkumpōtu liviṅ°ṭaṉi, aṉṟiya āṟṟal mikka kuḻuviṉar palarum etirkkavē maṟpaṭṭaṉar. pūyarkaḷ1 liviṅ°ṭaṉ taṅkaḷ aṭimi vāṇikatti aḻikkap pārkkiṉṟaṉar’eṉṟa ivarmītu cīṟiṉar. teṉāḵprika aracāṅkam ellā vallaracukaḷuṭaṉum oppuravu kāṭṭi ‘camayap paṇiyāḷar nāṭṭuppaṟṟu aṟṟavarāka naṭakkiṉṟaṉar’ eṉṟu eṇṇiṟṟu. ākavē avvaracāṅkattārum avarkaḷiṭam uvarppukkāṭṭiṉar. camayappaṇi niliyamō liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam camaya uṭkiḷiyiṉ kaṭṭuppāṭṭi mīṟip potu maṉappāṉmiyuṭaṉ naṭantuvantatu kaṇṭu puḻuṅkiṟṟu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ik kuṟukiya maṉappāṉmikaḷukkiṭiyum uṇmiyāṉa aruḷ nōkkuṭaṉ iṟivaṉ paṇiyil vīṟu koṇṭu iṟaṅkiṉār. 
putuppayiṟci
naṉṉampikki muṉiyil cila nāṭkaḷil, liviṅ°ṭaṉ āḵprikap payaṇattiṉpōtu tamakku ēṟ
paṭṭirunta uḷnākku vaḷarcciyi aṟuvi maruttuvattāl cariceytu koṇṭār. attuṭaṉ avar tikkaṟiyāta iṭaṅkaḷil tam niliyaṟiya utavumpaṭi kōḷkaḷiṉ nilikaḷiyum viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ nilikaḷiyum koṇṭu nilak kiṭakkiyi variyaṟiyiṭṭu aḷavip paṭam variyum maṟiyiyum payiṉṟu koṇṭār. 
pūyar aṭṭūḻiyam
liviṅ°ṭaṉ kurumāṉukku vantapōtu uḷḷan tuṭikkum nikaḻcci oṉṟik kēḷvippaṭa nērntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ paṇiyi veṟutta aṭimi vaṇikarākiya pūyarkaḷ ivar arum paṇi āṟṟiya iṭamākiya pekkuvāṉa nāṭṭit tākkic cūṟi koṇṭaṉar; seccīliṉ kuḻantikaḷ cilariyum, liviṅ°ṭaṉ paḷḷip piḷḷikaḷiyum, piṟamakḷiyum aṭimikaḷākak koṇṭaṉar, liviṅ°ṭaṉ kuṭiciyiyum kuṭiyiruppiyum aṭiyōṭu aḻittu oḻittaṉar. pekkuvāṉar celvamellām aḻintu palar uyir iḻantaṉar. eñciya cilaruṭaṉ seccīl liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam vantu muṟiyiṭṭār. uyirvatikku āḷāṉa akkāttum ammakkaḷ nampikkiyum uṟutiyum paṟṟum keṭāmal liviṅ°ṭaṉ paṇiyit tāmē naṭatta muṉvanta oṉṟē ivar maṉap puṇṇukku ōraḷavu āṟutalāka iruntatu.
nalla nōkkam
liviṅ°ṭaṉ seccīlit tammuṭaṉ aḻittuc
ceṉṟu avarukkum avaric cērntavarukkum ēṟpaṭṭa tīmiyippaṟṟi vikṭōriya pēraraciṭam muṟiyiṭa muyaṉ
ṟār. āṉāl pakkat tuṇiyiṉṟi atu vīṇāyiṟṟu. iṉi vaṭakkē cellum muyaṟciyil pūyar etirppu  irukkum eṉpati liviṅ°ṭaṉ aṟintār. eppāṭupaṭṭāyiṉum āḵprika uḷnāṭṭup pakutiyi nākarika ulakuṭaṉ iṇittālaṉṟi aṭimi vāṇikatti niṟutta muṭiyātu eṉpati ivar kaṇṭār. ātalāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ vaṭakku nōkki mīṇṭum payaṇamāṉār. immuṟi ivar cella virumpiya iṭam sepiṭṭuvēṉiṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakōṭiyil uḷḷa ‘liṉyāṇṭi’2 ākum.
pūyarkaḷuṭaṉ uṭaṉpaṭikki
pūyarkaḷukkum āḵprika makkaḷukkum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷākap perumpōr nikaḻntu vantatu. ippōrē ‘kā ḵpirip pōr’3 eṉappaṭum.  appōr kīḻkkarip
pakkam naṭantatu. ākavē avvaḻi eḷitāyiṉum avarkaḷ ati viṭṭakaṉṟu kalahārip pālivaṉattiṉ vaḻiyē mīṭṭum cellavēṇṭiyavarāyiṉar. appaṭic ceṉṟum pūyarkaḷuṭaṉ pala kikalappukaḷ nērntaṉa. pōr iṟutiyil piriṭṭiṣār appakutiyil paṅku koṇṭatālum ‘parōlāṅ’4 vakuppiṉar pekkuvāṉarkaḷuṭaṉ cērntataṉālum pūyarkaḷ uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytu koṇṭaṉar. seccīliṉ piḷḷikaḷil pūyarkaḷiṉ koṭumikku iriyākātu eñciya cilarum piṟa pekku vāṉarum tam iṉattavaruṭaṉ vantu cērntaṉar. ‘iṟantavar piṟantaṉar’ eṉṟa aḷavil pekkuvāṉar iṉpak kūttāṭiṉar. 
putiya iṭattil toṇṭupurital
vaḻiyil pala ciṟṟāṟukaḷ  veḷḷameṭuttup perukic cēṟāka irantataṉālum, vaḻikāṭṭa utaviya āṭkaḷ kalakam viḷittatiṉālum payaṇam mikat tāmatittatu. avarkaḷ ‘cōp’5 āṟṟi aṭintatum makōlolō vakuppiṉar irunta iṭam ceṉṟu cērntaṉar. sepiṭṭuvēṉukkup piṉ āṭcikku vanta avaṉ makaṉ, āṭcip poṟuppi virum
pātu vilaṅkiyataṉāl avaṉ tampiyākiya sekkeleṭṭu6 talivaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam naṭpuṭiyava
ṉākavē iruntu vantāṉ. āṉāl, pōrccukkīciya aṭimi vāṇikarkaḷ avaṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāṉākiya impepi7 yi avaṉukku etirākat tūṇṭi ātaravu aḷittu vantārkaḷ. liviṅ°ṭaṉ sekkeleṭṭi aṭimi vāṇikatti viṭṭoḻikkumpaṭi uṟutimoḻi kūṟuvittār. mēlum maṟṟa vakuppiṉariyum pōrccukkīciyariyum aṭimi vāṇikattik kiviṭṭu makōlolōkkaḷuṭaṉ kālnaṭi vāṇikam puriyumāṟu tūṇṭivantār. itaṉmūlam avarkaḷukku mēṟkil irunta ‘lecculāṭep’8 mutaliya vakuppiṉaruṭaṉ naṭpum vāṇikat toṭarpum mikuntaṉa. 
liviṅ°ṭaṉ liṉyāṉṭiyil cilanāḷ taṅki, maruttuvap paṇiyum camayap paṇiyum āṟṟivantār. aṅ
kuḷḷavarkaḷiṟ palar ēḻu allatu eṭṭu peṇkaḷi maṇantavarkaḷ. liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ camayatti mēṟkoṇ
ṭāl, oru maṉiviyit tavira maṟṟavari nīkkavēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭuppāṭu vēṇṭum. atu, avarkaḷi liviṅ°ṭaṉ camayattil cērāvaṇṇam neṭunāḷ taṭuttu vittatu. āyiṉum avarkaḷuṭiya potuvāḻvi liviṅ°ṭaṉāl pala vakikaḷil mēmpaṭutta muṭintatu. 
kāyccal nōy liṉpāṉṭiyilum mikutiyāka iruntatu. eṉavē liviṅ°ṭaṉum aṭikkaṭi nōyvāyp
paṭṭār. ivaruṭaṉ kuṭiyēṟiya mokōlolōkkaḷum varavarak kuṟiyalāyiṉar. eṉavē liviṅ°ṭaṉ mēl kaṭaṟkari ceṉṟu veḷināṭṭut toṭarpi vaḷarkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa tam paḻiya tiṭṭatti virivil toṭaṅkiṉār. ataṉpaṭi avar sekkeleṭṭu tanta āṭkaḷuṭaṉum, kālnaṭikaḷ, uṇavupporuḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉum 1853 ām āṇṭu navampar 11 l ‘lōvāṇṭā’  nōkkip puṟappaṭṭār. 
putiyavar utavi
liviṅ°ṭaṉ ceyta payaṇaṅkaḷ aṉittilum lōvāṇṭāp payaṇamē mikunta tollikaḷi uṭiyatu. ivar erutu oṉṟiṉ mītu ūrntu āṟṟik kaṭakka muṟpaṭṭār. appoḻutu anta erutu ivari naṭṭāṟṟil viṭṭu akaṉṟatu. sekeleṭṭuviṉ celvākku paraviya elliyik kaṭantapiṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ ivaric cērntōrum ‘pālōṇṭākkaḷ’9 eṉṟa vakuppiṉarik kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ talivaṉ ‘ṣiṉṭē’10 eṉpavaṉ avaṉ tuṇittaliviyāṉa ‘māṉeṉkō’11 eṉpavaḷ avaṉ kīḻirantu kiḻakkelliyi āṇṭāḷ. avaḷ āṇmiyum vīramum piṭivātamum uṭiya vīrap peṇmaṇi. avaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉ kūṭṭattārkku utava muṉvantāḷ. āyiṉum avaḷ avarkaḷi āṟṟil  paṭakumūlam pōkaviṭāmal taṉ maṉam pōl kari vaḻiyākap pōkac ceytāḷ. liviṅ°ṭaṉ āṭkaḷ avaḷuṭaṉ ottu naṭakka muṭiyāmal avalamuṟṟaṉar. 
ṣiṉṭē naṭu āḵprikāvilēyē mikka āravāramāṉa araca niliyuṭiyavaṉ. avaṉ taṉ kolu maṇṭapattil liviṅ°ṭaṉ avaṉukku ōr erutip paricaḷittār, piṉ makōlolōvuṭaṉ vāṇikam ceyyat tūṇṭiyapiṉ, avaṉ tanta vaḻikāṭṭikaḷuṭaṉ kātemā  eṉṟa maṟṟoru talivaṉi aṭintu aṅkum avaṉāl ātarikkappeṟṟār. 
pala iṭiyūṟukaḷ
itaṉ piṉ, liviṅ°ṭaṉ nōyālum kīḻuḷḷōr kalakaṅkaḷālum pala tollikaḷ aṭintār. palamuṟi tam āṭkaḷiyē tuppākkiyāl accuṟutti aṭakka
vēṇṭiyavarāṉār. ‘cīpōk’13 eṉṟa vakuppiṉariṉ talivaṉ paṭikaḷum avarkaḷi aḻittup peruṅ koḷḷipeṟa eṇṇiṉāṉ. liviṅ°ṭaṉum ivar āṭ
kaḷum kāṭṭiya āṇmiyāl  avaṉ paṇintuviṭṭāṉ. eṉiṉum pōkumiṭam eṅkum avaṉ āṭkaḷ tolli tantukoṇṭē iruntaṉar, iṟutiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉum ivaric cērntavarkaḷum ēpral mātam mutalām nāḷ ‘hōvāṅhō’14 eṉṟa āṟṟi aṇukiṉar. av
vāṟṟik kaṭakkap pōrccukīsiya ūrt talivaṉ oruvaṉiṉ utavi vēṇṭiṉar. avaṉum ivarkaḷiṭam ētēṉum oru poruḷik kimmāṟākap peṟa eṇṇi
ṉāṉ. āyiṉum  liviṅ°ṭaṉ mutaliyōr tam miṭamaḷḷa poruḷkaḷi iḻakka maṉamiṉṟi avaṉ āṭkaḷic caṭṭi ceyyāmal etirttup pōrāṭiṉar; iṟutiyil ‘sipriyāṉō’15 eṉṟa pōrccukīciya paṭittalivaṉ utaviyāl āṟṟik kaṭantaṉar. avaṉē avarkaḷukku uṇavum nalliṭamum tantatuṭaṉ mākōlolōkkaḷukku virivil ūtiyam tarum vāṇikamuṟikaḷik kāṭṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ utaviyālēyē avarkaḷ eḷitil eñciya tūrattiyum kaṭantu lōvāṇṭā ceṉṟārkaḷ.
lōvāṇṭāvil liviṅ°ṭaṉ
sekelēṭṭuviṉ paricukaḷuḷ iraṇṭu yāṉit tantaṅkaḷ nalla vilikku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. liviṅ°ṭaṉ apporuḷik koṇṭu avaṉukkuk kutirikaḷum, piṟa poruḷkaḷum vāṅkiṉar, avar mēlum naṭantu iṟutiyil kaṭalik kaṇṭaṉar. atuvariyil kaṭaliyē kaṇṭirāta makōlolōkkaḷ, kaṭalik kaṇṭatum aṭinta viyap
piṟku elli illi. lōvāṇṭāvil aṭimi  vāṇika etirppup paṇiyāḷarāṉa tiru. kepriyēl16 liviṅ°ṭaṉi
yum maṟṟumaḷḷavariyum mika aṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟār. liviṅ°ṭaṉukkuk kappal mālumi oruvarum naṉku paḻakka
māṉār. avaratu kappal mūlamākavē liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam
payaṇattil eṭutta paṭaṅkaḷi nila iyal ārāyccik kaḻakattiṟku aṉuppiṉār. āṉāl kālakkēṭṭiṉāl akkappal uṭintupōṉatāl avaṟṟi ivar mīṇṭum varintaṉuppa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
kaṇṇukku iṉiya kāṭci
pōrccukīsiya āḵprikāvil liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷ ivarukku makiḻcci aḷittaṉa. pōrccukīsiyar nāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaṉ taṅkutaṭiyiṉṟi maṇuṟavukoṇṭu kalantaṉar. nāṭṭu makkaḷum kalvi, aluval, āṭci im
mūṉṟilum veḷḷiyaruṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭuk kalantiruntaṉar. pūyarkaḷ nāṭṭumakkaḷi etirttatik kāṭṭilum āṅkilēyar pirittuvittu oppuravu kāṭṭiyati viṭa - iḵtu evvaḷavō iṉiya kāṭci eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ karutiṉār. 
muṉ iṭattiṟku mīḷal
tirumpum payaṇattil cīpōkkukaḷ muṉṉaḷavu tolli taravilli. ‘kaṭamā’17 ‘viṉṭō’18 ākiyavarkaḷ uṟaviṉarpōl kaḷippuṭaṉ varavēṟṟaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉum tām koṇṭuvanta paḻaṅkaḷ, uṇavupporuḷkaḷ, iṉpa vāḻvup poruḷkaḷ, ākiyavaṟṟāl avarkaḷi makiḻvittār. makōlolōkkaḷ, iṟantavar mīṇṭum vantālotta makiḻcci koṇṭukūttāṭi, etirkoṇṭu aḻittaṉar. avarkaḷāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ, ūrvalam varum aracakumaraṉ pōl parōṭsāp paḷḷattākkiliruntu19 liṉyāṉṭivari āravāramākak koṇṭu cellappaṭṭār.
vikṭōriyā nīrvīḻcci
liṉyāṉṭiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ eṭṭu vāraṅkaḷ taṅkiṉār. ‘uḷnāṭṭup pakutiyi mēlkaṭaṟkariyuṭaṉ iṇittāy viṭṭatu. iṉik kiḻakkup pakkattuṭaṉum iṇippu ēṟpaṭuttavēṇṭum’ eṉa eṇṇiya liviṅ°ṭaṉ, kiḻakkē kilimēṉ20 nōkkip puṟappaṭṭār.  avarkaḷuṭaṉ sekeleṭṭu jāmpesit talinilam vari vantāṉ. liviṅ°ṭaṉum piṟarum avvāṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu atil uḷḷa oru periya nīrvīḻcciyi aṭintaṉar. ipperiya nīrvīḻcciyiliruntu nīrttivalikaḷ mika uyarntu tūṇkaḷ
pōl mēleḻuntu ceṉṟaṉa. ākavē, ataṉi annāṭṭu makkaḷ mōsi- vā-tuṉyā21, aḵtāvatu ‘iriccaliṭum nīrāvi’ eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam nāṭṭi, annāḷ āṇṭa vikṭōriyā pēraraciyiṉ peyari iṭṭu, ataṉi ‘vikṭōriyā nīrvīḻcci’22 eṉṟa aḻittaṉar. aḵtu iṉṟaḷavum appeyarālēyē vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. 
putiya kāṭcikaḷ
annīrvīḻcciyiṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷiyum aṭarntu uyarnta maraṅkaḷ aḻakuceytaṉa. teṉ āḵprikāvil liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷ ellām paranta pālivaṉaṅkaḷum putarkaḷum catuppu nilaṅkaḷum maṅkalāṉa tōṟṟamuṭiya kuṉṟukaḷumē ām. āṉāl itaṟku māṟāka, liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṇmuṉ iyaṟki, paṉṉiṟat tōci virittāṭum pacci mayilpōl taṉ muḻu aḻakuṭaṉ tōṟṟamaḷitta mutaliṭam ituvē. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ciṟu paṭakukaḷil ceṉṟu nīr vīḻcciyi aṭuttu uḷḷa tīvil iṟaṅkiṉār. ataṉuḷ pāṟiyit tuḷittōṭum kiḷi nīr vīḻccikaḷik kaṇṭu viyantār. parutta tūṇkaḷ uruvil taṇṇīr nilattik kiḻittu pātāḷattil celvatu pōṉṟa akkāṭci accan taruvatāka iruntatu. 
kiḻakkup pakuti kuṭikaḷ
mēṟṟiciyil vāḻnta makkaḷippōlavē kīḻt
ticiyilum palavaki muraṭṭu vakuppiṉar tolli tara iruntaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ cila camayam accuṟuttalālum, cila camayam nayattālum, cila camayam tammiṭattiluḷḷa ariya nākarikap poruḷkaḷāṉa kaṭikāram, veyil kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāl vēṭikki kāṭṭuvatālum avarkaḷit tam vayappaṭuttik koṇṭār. pōrccukīsiyaruṭaṉ aṇmivarip pōriṭṭa makkaḷ cilar, liviṅ°ṭaṉip pōrccukkīsiyareṉa niṉittup pakittaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam tōliṉ tūya veṇmiyik kāṭṭit tām vēṟu nāṭṭiṉar eṉpati uṇarttiṉār. avarkaḷ “ā! tūya veṇṇiṟam kaṟuppuṭaṉ naṭpuṭiyatu; kalappu niṟamē eṅkaḷip pakippatu” eṉṟa kūṟi naṭpukkoṇṭārkaḷ. 
liviṅ°ṭaṉ pōrccukīsiya elli aṭintatum, mēlip pōrccukkīsiyar cilariṭamiruntu peṟṟa kaṭitaṅkaḷ avarukku mējar hikkārṭu23 eṉpavar; avar liviṅ°ṭaṉ mutaliyōrukku uṇavu aṉuppi varavēṟṟār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tammuṭaṉ vanta makōlelōkkaḷi iṅkē uḷḷa ṭeṭṭi24 eṉṟa iṭattil taṅkac ceytaṉar, piṟaku kaṭal vaḻiyāka seṉṉāvukkum25 aṅkiruntu muriṭiya
sukkum26 ceṉṟār. ivaruṭaṉ ceṉṟa ‘sekvēpu’27 eṉṟa makōlolō vakuppiṉaṉ kaṭal, kappal mutaliya kaṇkāṇāk kāṭciyik kaṇṭa maṭṭaṟṟa makiḻcciyāl nilitaṭumāṟip pittaṉāki iruntāṉ. maṟṟa makōlo
lōkkaḷ viṟaku veṭṭi tal mutaliya toḻilil īṭupaṭṭu, ṭeṭṭiyilēyē taṅkip poruḷ īṭṭi iṉpuṭaṉ vāḻntaṉar. 
liviṅ°ṭaṉ muriṭiyasil oru tiṅkaḷ taṅkiṉār. āḵprikap payaṇattiṉimiyē nōyppaṭṭu uppiyirunta tamatu nuriyīrali ivar aṟuvi maruttuvattāl kuṇap
ṭuttik koṇṭār. ataṉpiṉ ‘pi’ aṇṭ ‘ō’ kaḻakattiṟku uriya kaṇṭiyā28 eṉṟa kappalil tāy nāṭu cellap puṟap
paṭṭār.

12. kiḻakku āḵprikā
tāynāṭṭil ciṟappup peṟutal
liviṅ°ṭaṉ payaṇam ceyta kappal, iṅkilāntu cellum vaḻiyil uṭintupōyiṟṟu. ataṉāl ivar, vēṟu kappalēṟi mārcēl°1, pāri° ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka vantu cērntār. tāy nāṭṭil makkaḷ āyirak
kaṇakkil tiraḷ tiraḷāka vantu ivarik kaṇṭaṉar. maṉṉar nila iyal ārāyccik kaḻakam2, laṇṭaṉ camayap paṇik kaḻakam3 mutaliya kaḻakaṅkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉukkup pārāṭṭukaḷ aḷittu, paricukaḷum, taṅkappatakkaṅkaḷum tantaṉa. laṇṭaṉ, taṉ nakara urimiyi ivarukkut taṅkap pēḻiyil vittu aḷittatu. kiḷā°kō nakaram, urimi aḷittu 2000 poṉṉum tantatu, eṭiṉparō, līṭ°,  livarpūl, pirmiṅhām mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷil pārāṭṭukaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. āk°ḵpōrṭu, kēmpiriṭj palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, aṟiñar paṭṭaṅkaḷ nalkiṉa. ivvaḷaviṟkum mēlāka annāḷiya āṅkilanāṭṭu mutaṉ mantiriyāṉa pāmar°ṭaṉ4 liviṅ°ṭaṉik kiḻakku āḵppirikāviṉ ayalnāṭṭu amiccarāka5 amarttip perumippaṭuttiṉār. 
pirayāṇam paṟṟiya mutalnūl
liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ tāynāṭṭu naṇparkaḷ peruntaki ār. marccisaṉ 6, tiru . jāṉ marē 7 eṉpōrāvar. ivarkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉi nōkki, “tāṅkaḷ mīṇṭum āḵprikā
vukkup payaṇamākumuṉ,  muṉceyta āḵprikap payaṇaṅ
kaḷip paṟṟi oru nūl eḻutavēṇṭum” eṉa vēṇṭiṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉum avvaṟē eḻutiṉar. innūl ivarukku nalla varuvāy tantatu. 
āḵprikā mīḷal
itukāṟum uṭal nalattiṉ poruṭṭum kuḻantiyiṉ kalviyiṉ poruṭṭum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭamiruntu pirintu, iṅkilāntil taṅkiyirunta tiruvāṭṭi liviṅ°ṭaṉ, iṉimēlum pirintirukka maṉamiṉṟi, uṭaṉcella uṟuti koṇṭār. eṉavē avarkaḷ, mūtta piḷḷikaḷ mūvari
yum tāynāṭṭil taṅkacceytu, kaṭicip putalvaṉāṉa ā°veluṭaṉ8 1859 ām āṇṭu mārc 10 il perl (muttu) eṉṟa kappalil āḵprikāvukkup payaṇamāyiṉar. 
uṭaṉ ceṉṟa ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ
veḷināṭṭu amiccar eṉṟa muṟiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ ērāḷamāṉa āṭkaḷum taḷavāṭaṅkaḷum aṉuppa aracāṅkam eṇṇik koṇṭiruntatu. āṉāl, ‘uṇmip paṇikku āravāram kālkaṭṭu ākum’ eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ eṇṇiṉār. ākavē avar aṟiviyal vallunar cilaruṭaṉ ciṟcila poraḷkaḷi maṭṭum ēṟṟu, cilavaṟṟi ēṟka maṟuttuviṭṭār. avvallunarkaḷuḷ aṟiñarkark9 eṉṟa ceṭinūṟ pulamiyāḷar10 tiru. si. liviṅ°ṭaṉ eṉṟa maṇkūṟṟu nūlaṟiñar 11 avarukkut tuṇiyāka tiru. ār. tārṉṭaṉ12 eṉṟa maṟṟoruvar ākiya mūvar iṭam
peṟṟaṉar. aṉivarum sīrālōṉik13 kaṭantu naṉ
ṉampikki muṉivarumuṉṉar liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ maṉivi mīṭṭum uṭal nalivuṟṟār. eṉavē, liviṅ°ṭaṉ avari avar peṟṟōriṭam viṭṭu, mēṟceṉṟu kiḻakku āḵprikak kariyic cerntār. 
ārāycci ceytal
liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ naṉṉampikki aracāṅka aṟiviyal vallunar cilar ceṉṟaṉar, liviṅ°ṭaṉ avarkaḷiṉ utavi
yāl ḵjāmpasi14 yiṉ viḻuvāyil piriyum nāṉku kāl
kaḷiyum naṉku ārāyntaṉar; avaṟṟuḷ ‘koṅkōṉ’15 eṉṟa kiḷiyit tērntaṉar. piṉ avarkaḷ āṟṟup payaṇattiṟkeṉat tāṅkaḷ koṇṭuvantirunta ‘mārāparṭ’16 eṉṟa marakkalattiṉ piritta pakutikaḷi mīṭṭum iṇittu, akkiḷiyāṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē mēl nōkkic ceṉṟu, ṭeṭṭiyi aṭintaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ aṅkē muṉṉar viṭṭuc ceṉṟa makōlolōkkaḷik kaṇṭu makiḻcci aṭintaṉar. 
nayāsā ēri
ataṉpiṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam naṇparuṭaṉ ḵjāmpesi
yiṉ maṟṟoru kiḷiyākiya ‘ṣīrē’17 āṟṟi ārāyn
tuṇarum eṇṇattuṭaṉ payaṇam ceytār. avarkaḷ iṅkē ṭiṅkēṉ18 sipisā19 mutaliya nāṭṭut talivarkaḷuṭaṉ naṭpup pūṇṭu iṟutiyil ēpral 18il ‘ṣirvā’20 eṉṟa ēriyik kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar. ataṉpiṉ avarkaḷ mīṇṭum kaṭaṟkari vantu cērntaṉar, paṭaku ceppaṉiṭappaṭṭatu; piṉ makōlolōkkaḷ cilari vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu, mēlum vaṭakkē payaṇam ceytu cepṭampar 16 l ‘nayāsā’ ēriyik21 kaṇṭaṉar. ivviraṇṭu ērikaḷiyum mutalmutal kaṇṭa āṅkilēyarkaḷ avarkaḷēyāvar. 
iṉpamum tuṉpamum
‘nayāsā’ ēri 200 kal aḷavu nīṇṭu kiṭantatu. ataṉic cuṟṟilum uḷḷa iṭam mikac ceḻippāka iruntatu. ivviṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa makkaḷ perumpālum amitiyāṉa vāḻkki uṭiyavarkaḷē yāvar. āyiṉum arāpiyar vaṭakkiliruntu iṅku vantu naṭattiya aṭimi vāṇikattiṉ kāraṇamāka avarkaḷiṭiyē accamum, aiyappāṭum, pūcalum niṟintiruntaṉa. liviṅ°ṭaṉ kiḻakkāḵprikkāvil taṅkiya kālattil palamuṟi inta ēriyi aṇukip poṟumiyōṭu muṟṟilum ārāyntār; atippaṟṟiya paṭaṅkaḷum vivaraṅkaḷum cērttār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ aṭimi vāṇikatti niṟutti ippakkaṅkaḷil amitiyum, nalvāḻvum ēṟpaṭutta evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum am muyaṟcikaḷ ellām  appoḻutikku vīṇāyiṉa. mēlum, avarkaḷ cuvaṭṭip piṉpaṟṟip pōrccukīsiyar pukuntu aṭimi vāṇikatti vaḷarkkavum nērntatu. ataṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ mutalil cellumpōtu naṟuñcōlikaḷāka viḷaṅkiya iṭaṅkaḷ, maṟumuṟi celkiyil kaṇṇīrum tuyarum mikka ‘naṉkaṭāy’t tikaḻntaṉa. āyiṉum nāḷaṭivil āṅkila āṭciyum nākarikamum pukuntu aṭimi aṟavē oḻiyum vakiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ paṇi, oru vaḻikāṭṭiyākavē amintatu. 
ṭeṭṭiyil taṅkiya makōlolōkkaḷil palar, ammi nōyāl tollippaṭṭu iṟantaṉar. avarkaḷi mīṭṭum tam nāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu varuvatāka liviṅ°ṭaṉ sekeleṭṭukku uṟuti kūṟiyiruntār. ataṉpaṭi ivar avarkaḷi uṭaṉ koṇṭu ḵjāmpesi āṟṟil mēl nōkkic ceṉṟu ‘ṣeṣeke’23 eṉṉum iṭattil sekeleṭṭik kaṇṭaṉar. avaṉ toḻunōykaṇṭu taṉ makkaḷiṭamiruntu pirintiruntāṉ. avaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ aṟivurikaḷi maṟantu, makkaḷiṭi vēṟṟumi kāṭṭiṉāṉ; taṉ tanti amitta pēraraciyum avaṉ cīrkuliyaviṭṭiruntāṉ. liviṅ°ṭaṉ avaṉ nōykku maruntu tantum  maṟṟilum kuṇam ēṟpaṭavilli. āṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl avaṉ maṉaamiti peṟṟāṉ. makololōkkaḷ iḻanta tāyi mīṭṭum peṟṟa cēykaḷ pōla liviṅ°ṭaṉi varavēṟṟu makiḻcci eytiṉārkaḷ. 
naṟpaṇiyil tōlvi
avarkaḷ maṟupaṭiyum ṭeṭṭikku vantaṉar. ap
pōtum āṅkila nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ cārpil ōr āṅkila camayap paṇikkuḻām aṅku aṉuppappaṭṭiruntatu. avarkaḷikkaṇṭa liviṅ°ṭaṉ peru makiḻcci aṭintār. liviṅ°ṭaṉum  ikkuḻāttiṉarum oppaṟṟa nalleṇṇattuṭaṉ ottuḻittu āḵprika makkaḷi īṭēṟṟavum, ottuḻittu āḵprika makkaḷi īṭēṟṟavum, aṭimiyi oḻikkavum arumpāṭupaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl iṟivaṉ tiruviḷiyāṭal ‘ippōtu avarkaḷukku etirāka iruntatu. avarkaḷ naṟpaṇiyiṉ tōlvi, liviṅ°ṭaṉ pukaḻi anniliyil paḻippuyalukku āḷākkiyatu; iṭiyūṟum viḷivittu viṭṭatu. ‘maṅkañcā’23 eṉṟa oru vakuppiṉar liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam naṭpuk koṇṭaṉar; ‘makōmerō’24 eṉṟa ūril camayappaṇi niliyam amikka iṭantantaṉar. iṅkilāntiliruntu vanta camayak kuḻattiṉ talivar ivviṭattiliruntu paṇiyāṟṟa
lāṉār, āṉāl maṅkañcākkaḷi ‘ajāvā’25 eṉṟa vakuppiṉar aṭikkaṭi tākkiṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ip
pōrkaḷil īṭupaṭa virumpavilli. ivar camayat talivariyum ivvakiyil eccarittiruntār. āṉāl ajāvākkaḷāl naṉmakkaḷ tuṉpuṟuvatik kāṇappeṟāta camayattalivar, maṅkañcākkaḷ pakkam niṉṟu utaviṉar. ippōrkaḷil īṭupaṭṭum, nōyvāyppaṭṭum camayattalivar kuḻāttil oruvar nīṅka maṟṟavar aṉivarum iṟantaṉar. 
mīṇṭuvara āṇi
ṣīrēyiṉ vaṭakkilirunta “rōvumā’26 āṟṟi ārāyvatil īṭupaṭṭiruntu mīṇṭa liviṅ°ṭaṉ ic ceytiyik kēṭṭu maṉam uṭintār. piṉ kaṭaṟkariyil vantu cērntatum aṭutta kappalil vanta celvi makkeṉsi27 mutaliya naṇparkaḷik kaṇṭār; avar koṇṭirunta kaṭitam mūlam aracāṅkattār tam paṇiyiṉiṉṟu tammi nīkki mīṇṭuvarap paṇittatāka aṟintār. 
maṉiviyār iṟappu
‘paṭṭa kālilē paṭum, keṭṭa kuṭiyē keṭum’ eṉṟa mutumoḻippaṭi ittuyarac ceytiyi aṭuttu liviṅ°ṭaṉ vāḻvil maṟṟōr iṭiyum vīḻntatu. celvi makkeṉsiyuṭaṉ naṉṉampikkiyil cila nāḷ iṉpa vāḻvil kulaviyirunta liviṅ°ṭaṉ maṉiviyār, avvāṇṭu ēpral 27 il tiṭumeṉa maṟivuṟṟār. āyiram muṟi emaṉi etirttup pōriṭṭa āṇ ciṅkamāṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ, appōtu ciṟu piḷḷipōl tēmpit tēmpi aḻutaṉarām. 
kalaṅkā uḷḷam
liviṅ°ṭaṉukku iṭiyil vanta pukaḻc celviyum, ivar nūlāl vanta tiruccelviyum ivari viṭṭu nīṅkiṉar. ippōtu ivar varuvāy aṟṟuk kaṭaṉ paṭṭār. in
niliyil ivar, tammiṭamirunta ‘nayasā’ eṉṟa ciṟu kappali viṟka eṇṇip pampāykkuc ceṉṟār. aṅku viṟka muṭiyātu pōkavē, mīṇṭum iṅkilāntu pōyc cērntār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ maṟumuṟiyum pampāykkuc ceṉṟu akkappali aḻiviliyāka (naṣṭamāka)2600 poṉṉukku viṟṟār. āyiṉ antō! apporuḷum ivarukkuk kiṭṭāmal, apporuḷi viṭṭuvitta intiyap poruḷ niliyam muṭivuṟṟatām! ittaṉi tīmikaḷiṭiyēyum maṉaṅ kalaṅkātu tam paṇiyilē uṟuti tavaṟātirunta liviṅ°ṭaṉ maṉa urattiṉ taṉmiyi eṉṉeṉpatu! ‘meyttiru vantuṟṟapōtum ventuyarvantuṟṟa pōtum, ottirukkum uḷḷattu uravōr’ eṉṟa periyār iyalpiṟku ivar ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭāyiṉar.

13. mūṉṟām pirayāṇam
aḻittamikkuk kāraṇam
liviṅ°ṭaṉ iṅkilāntukkup pōṉa āṇṭu, pāmar°ṭaṉ perumakaṉār ‘amiccar kuḻu’ āṭciyiṉ iṟuti āṇṭāka iruntatu. pāmar°ṭaṉ eppōtumpōla avar mītu viruppam uṭiyavarākavē iruntār. āyiṉum, ‘pōrccukīsiya aracāṅkam uṅkaḷ ceyalkaḷ eṅkaḷukku māṟāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉālēyē uṅkaḷi mīṭṭum aḻikka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu’ eṉṟa avar kūṟiṉār. pāmar°ṭaṉ perumāṭṭi liviṅ°ṭaṉukkut tēnīr viruntaḷittup pārāṭṭiṉār. 
iraṇṭām pirayāṇam paṟṟiya nūl
liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ, °kāṭlāntiṉ paḻam peruṅ kuṭiyiṉaruḷ oruvarāṉa arkil kōmāṉ1 aḻippi ēṟṟu avarūrākiya iṉverāri2 ceṉṟār. aṅkē akkōmāṉiṉ tūṇṭutalāl tam iraṇṭām muṟip payaṇam kuṟittu nūl oṉṟu eḻutiṉār
aṭimi vāṇikam oḻiyavēṇṭum
1864 ām āṇṭu iliyutirkālattil ‘pāt’3 eṉṉum iṭattil liviṅ°ṭaṉ pēruri āṟṟiṉār. appōtu, ‘aṭimi vāṇikattil mūḻki aḻiyum kikkāḵprika makkaḷi īṭēṟṟum  paṇiyil āṅkila iḷiñar peruvāriyāka muṉiyavēṇṭum”eṉak kūṟiṉār. akkūṟṟu, pōrckīsiya araciyalār pakimiyi iṉṉum perukkuvati ivar naṉku aṟivār. āyiṉum naṟkāriyattil yāvar pakimiyiyum ivar poruṭpaṭuttiyatilli. itaṉ payaṉāka ataṉpiṉ āḵprikāvil ivar paṇiyellām pōrccukīsiyariṉ kikkeṭṭāt tolivilirukkavēṇṭi vantatu. 
‘putunilam kāṇpavar’ patavi
ittakiya araciyal cikkalkaḷ iṉi liviṅ°ṭaṉ paṇiyit tākkak kūṭātu eṉṟa nalleṇṇattuṭaṉ, nila iyalārāyccik kaḻakattic cārnta ṭāṭarikkerun
takiyār4, putunilaṅkāṇpavar5 eṉṟa muṟiyilēyē ivari aṉuppa ēṟpāṭu ceytār. immuṟi liviṅ°ṭaṉ ārāyccikku uṭpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ rōvumā āṟu, taṅkaṉīkā ēri 6 ākiyaviyēyākum. ippakutiyik kaṭaṟkari vaḻikaḷālō allatu vaṭakkē nil āṟṟup7 pakutiyuṭaṉō iṇikka vaḻikāṇum paṭi liviṅ°ṭaṉ vēṇṭappaṭṭār. avar payaṇam toṭaṅkumuṉ araciyal pēraviyil varuṅkālap paṇipaṟṟiyum tam kōṭpāṭu paṟṟiyum pēciṉār. piṉ āk°ḵpōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattil  pēcikkoṇṭirukkum pōtu ivar tam tāyār maṟivuṟṟatākak kēṭṭu iṟutik kaṭaṉic ceṉṟu āṟṟiṉār. 
vaṟumiyē tōḻaṉ
liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ payaṇac celaviṟkāka aracāṅkattār 500 poṉṉum, nila ārāyccik kaḻakattār 500 poṉṉum, naṇparoruvar 1000 poṉṉum tantaṉar. ayalnāṭṭu amiccar eṉṟa peyar iruntum. appōtu ataṟku ūtiyam illātatāl ittoki ivarukkup pōtātākavē iruntatu. ‘nayasā’ kappal viṟṟa ciṟu tokiyi pampāyil iḻanta piṉ ivar tuṇintu vaṟumiyiyē tamatu tōḻaṉākakkoṇṭu āḵprikāvukkuc cella eṇṇiṉār. 
āḵprikāvi aṭital
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, peṉkuviṉ8 eṉṟa kappalil payaṇam toṭaṅkiṉār. intiyāvil nācikkiliruntu uṭaṉvanta orucila toṇrkaḷuṭaṉ mārc tiṅkaḷiṉ iṟutiyil ‘rōvumā’ viṉ kariyil iṟaṅkiṉār. ivar kuḻāttil iruntavar ēṟakkuṟiya muppattaṟuvar āvar. āḵprikat taṭpa veppa niliyilum aṅkuḷḷa ‘ceṭci’9 eṉṟa eṟumpuvakiyiṉiṭiyilum evvev vilaṅkukaḷ vāḻamuṭiyum eṉṟu kāṇa, liviṅ°ṭaṉ intiyāviliruntu  oṭṭikaḷ, erumikaḷ, kaḻutikaḷ, kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭuvantiruntār. 
arāpiyar tolli
āka°ṭu 8 il liviṅ°ṭaṉ nayāsā ēriyi aṭintār. arāpiyar ivari muṟṟilum pakittu utuk
kiṉar. ēriyiṉ paṭakukaḷ aṉittum avarkaḷ kiyila iruntamiyāl, liviṅ°ṭaṉ ēriyik kaṭakka muṭiyāmal , tammic cērntavaruṭaṉ teṟkup pakkamākac cuṟṟic ceṉṟār. arāpiyarkaḷ, āḵprikat talivarkaḷi oruvark
ketirāka oruvarit tūṇṭivantataṉāl ṣīrē āṟu ēriyiliruntu veḷivarum iṭattil nilimi mika mōcamāka iruntatu. avarkaḷiṉ tūṇṭutalālē liviṅ°ṭaṉ kuḻāttiluḷḷa palar maṉaṅkakeṭṭu liviṅ°ṭaṉiviṭṭu ḵjāṉsipārukkuc 10 ceṉṟaṉar aṅkuc ceṉṟa avarkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉ iṟantuviṭṭatākak katikaṭṭi viṭṭārkaḷ. ataṉik kēḷvippaṭṭa iṅkilāntu nila ārāyccik kaḻakam uṇmiyik kaṇṭaṟiya ‘yaṅ’11 eṉpavari aṉuppiṟṟu, piṟaku yaṅ eḻutiya aṟikkimūlam akkaḻakam uṇmiyi uṇarntatu. 
taṅkaṉīkā ēri
liviṅ°ṭaṉ itaṉ piṉ vaṭamēṟkākat ‘taṅkaṉīkā’ ēri nōkkic ceṉṟār. uṇavup poruḷkaḷ celavāy
viṭṭatāl nāṭṭac cōḷamum āṭṭup pālum maṭṭumē aruntum nilimi ēṟpaṭṭatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, āṭkaḷ illāmiyāl mūṭṭi cumakkak kūli āḷ amarttiṉar. itaṟkum kiyilirunta paṇam pōtavilli. ṭicampar tiṅkaḷil liviṅ°ṭaṉē nōyvāyppaṭṭār. ivariṉ kālnaṭikaḷum maruntukaḷum aṭikkaṭi tiruṭupōyiṉa. ittaṉi tolli kaḷukku iṭiyil, liviṅ°ṭaṉ ēpralil liyempā ēriyi12 aṭintār. iḵtu uṇmiyil taṅkaṉīkā ēriyiṉ teṉkōṭiyē eṉpatu piṉṉāl teriyavantatu. 
moyirō paṅviyōlō ērikaḷ
liyempāp pakutiyil pōrppuyal parantiruntatāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ teṟkunōkkit tirumpiṉār. nil āṟṟut taliyiṭamākak karutappaṭṭa moyirō ēriyi 13 nāṭiṉār. ‘upuṅku’ eṉṟa kuḻuviṉar liviṅ°ṭaṉip peruntaṉmi
yuṭaṉ naṭattiṉar. ‘mukamatu mokārip’14 eṉṉum aṭimi vaṇikaṉ arāyiṉāka iruntum liviṅ°ṭaṉukku vaḻit
tuṇivaṉāka iruntu utaviṉāṉ. avaṉ aḷitta viruntu, neṭunāḷ nallaṇavu illātirunta liviṅ°ṭaṉukku amutamāka iruntatu. 1867 ām āṇṭu navampar 8 il liviṅ°ṭaṉum maṟṟiyōrum mōyirō ēriyi aṭintārkaḷ. inta ēri 40 kal akalam uṭiyatāka iruntatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ataṉi naṉku ārāyntār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ippōtu mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa ēriyiṉ teṟkil ataṉiṉum periteṉak koḷḷap paṭṭa paṅviyōlō15 eṉṉum ēṟiyikkāṇa mīṭṭum teṟkē ceṉṟār. mokārip itaṉit taṭukkavum etirkkavum muyaṉṟum payaṉ paṭavilli. liviṅ°ṭaṉ juli 18 il anta ēriyik kaṇṭu ataṉ aḻakiya parappi nōkki makiḻntār. ataṉpiṉ ivar mīṇṭum arāpiyaruṭaṉ vantu cērntār. 
kāṅkōviṉ kiḷiyāṟu
liviṅ°ṭaṉ luvālāpā āṟu16 nil āṟṟil ceṉṟu cērum kiḷitāṉā eṉṟu kāṇa virumpiṉār. eṉavē, ivar luvālāpā āṟṟiṉ vāyākac ceṉṟu akṭōpar 25 il maṉiyūmākkaḷ17 nāṭṭiṉ talinakarāṉa pampār18 eṉṉum iṭatti aṭintār. iṅkut tam āṭkaḷukkāvum kaṭitaṅkaḷuk kākavum cilanāḷ kāttiruntār. iṟutiyil āṭkaḷil oru cilarē vantaṉar. kaṭitaṅkaḷil 40 varāmal taṭukkappaṭṭu, oṉṟē vantu cērntatu. vanta makkaḷum aḷavillāta maṟiyil campaḷam kōrip payaṇattit taṭukkap pārttārkaḷ. liviṅ°ṭaṉ, payattālum nayattālum varkaḷiccamāḷittuk koṇṭu ḵpipravariyil puṟappaṭṭac ceṉṟār. ataṉ payaṉāka luvālāpā āṟu niliṉ kiḷiyāṟu aṉṟu; kāṅkōviṉ kiḷiyē eṉpatu uṟutippaṭṭatu. 
liviṅ°ṭaṉum aṭimikaḷum
liviṅ°ṭaṉ, aṭimikaḷi viṭuvikka muyalpava
rāyiṉum cilacamayam aṭimikaḷ naṭatti ivar maṉattip puṇpaṭuttuvatāka iruntatu. aṭimikaḷ, tāṅkaḷ aṭimi
yāka irukkumpōtē taṅkaḷ talivarkaḷ muṉṉiliyil, tamakku uṇavu viṟṟa vaṇikarkaḷi aṭikkavum avarkaḷ kuhntikaḷip puṭikkavum tuṇintār. aṭimit taḷiyi akaṟṟap pāṭupaṭṭa vīrarākiya liviṅ°ṭaṉ, tuppākki nīṭṭi avvaṭimikaḷi accuṟuttit tirutta vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ivariṭam aṉuppap paṭṭavarkaḷ piriṭṭiṣ kuṭimakkaḷāṉa paṉiyarkaliṉ19 aṭimikaḷ āvar. avarkaḷum poy, cūtu, vaṅcam, koṭumi ākiyavaṟṟukku uṟiviṭamāka iruntu, peruntolli koṭuttaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉiviṭac caṟṟuk kuṟinta aruḷuḷm uṭiyavarkaḷ camayappaṇit talivarkaḷāka iruppaṉ, itaṉikkaṇṭu, ‘avarkaḷiṉ aṭimittaḷi cariyāṉatē’ eṉṟam kūṟiyiruppar. āṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ ikkoṭumikaḷic ceyalil kaṇṭittukkoṇṭē, maṉattil avarkaḷiṉ aṉmika iḻinilikku iraṅkiṉār. muṟitavaṟit tavaṟiḻikkum taṉiyaṉit tanti oṟukkumpōtum tantiyiṉ aṉpu māṟātaṉṟō?
aṟivaṟṟa aṭimikaḷ
liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ vēliyāṭkaḷ viṭutali peṟṟa aṭimikaḷāyiṉum ivariyē kollac caticeytaṉar. ‘apeṭ’20 eṉṟa oruvaṉ uṇmiyuḷḷavaṉāka iruntu muṉṉeccarikki tantatiṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ viḻippāka iruntār.  ivar, avarkaḷi vēliyilirantu nīkka muṉ
vantatum avarkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉ kālil viḻuntu maṉṉippup peṟṟaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ maṉṉippukku uriyavarallar eṉpatu virivil teriyavantatu. ṭukumpe21 eṉṟa oruvar arāpiya naṇpar cilaruṭaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉik kaṇṭaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉukka nāṭṭil paṭaku kiṭikkātatiṉ kāraṇam avvēliyāṭkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉip paṟṟip pollāṅku kūṟip paḻi parappiyatē eṉṟu avar kāṭṭiṉar. ataṉpiṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉ avarkaḷi vēliyiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟi, ṭukumpe eṉpavariṭam 2000 veḷḷi (400 poṉ) koṭuttup pattu āṭkaḷ peṟṟār. 
°ṭāṉliyiṉ varuki
liviṅ°ṭaṉ mīṇṭum  ujjiyi aṭintār iṅkē arāpiyarum piṟarum ivari naṇparāka naṭattiṉar. eṉiṉum palar ivar poruḷkaḷit tiruṭiṉar. uṭalum taḷarntu uḷḷattil uramum citinta innērattil ‘hū si’22 eṉpavar liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam vantu ōr ā‘kilēyar vantiruppatāka aṟivittār. āṉāl, vantavar liviṅ°ṭaṉ naṇpar °ṭāṉliyē23 yāvar. avar uṇmiyil amerikkā ‘niyūyārk herālaṭu ’eṉṟa amerikkac ceytittāḷ naṭattuvōrāl aṉuppappaṭṭavar. avar aṉuppappaṭṭataṉ nōkkam liviṅ°ṭaṉit tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭikka utavuvatum iṟantiruntārāyiṉ pārāṭṭuṭaṉ liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ elumpukaḷi eṭuttuvaruvatumē yākum. liviṅ°ṭaṉi uyiruṭaṉ kaṇṭa avar makiḻccikku elliyēyilli. liviṅ°ṭaṉum avarik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ ‘karikāṇāp puyaṟkaṭaliṟ puṇikaṇṭāṟ pōla’ aḷavilā uvaki koṇṭār, ēṉeṉil avar varavu liviṅ°ṭaṉ vāḻvil oru putiya kōḷiṉ varavu eṉṟē kūṟattakkatāka iruntatu. tuṉpamikka avaruṭiya piṟkāla vāḻvil ata pēramitiyi uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu. 
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

14. liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ maṟivu
uriya kālattil utavi
liviṅ°ṭaṉukku uriya neṭunāḷiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ oru paṇiyāḷiṭamiruntaṉa. °ṭāṉli liviṅ°ṭaṉik kāṇa varumpōtu avvēliyāḷiyum aḻittu vantiruntār. akkaṭitaṅkaḷuḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉ makkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. iviyaṉṟi °ṭāṉli vēṟu naṟceytikaḷum koṇṭuvantirantār. aḵtāvatu piriṭṭiṣ aracāṅkam mīṭṭum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ paṇikaḷi;ṉa arumi perumikaḷi uṇarntu ivarukku 1000 poṉ tara iṇaṅkiyatu eṉpatē
yākum. °ṭāṉli koṇarnta poraḷkaḷāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ uṭalniliyum celva niliyum uyarvuṟṟaṉa, ceytikaḷāl uḷaniliyum cemmiyuṟṟatu. 
iruvarum ceyta ārāycci
liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṇṭaṟiya vēṇṭumeṉa āval koṇṭa ceytikaḷ iraṇṭu. oṉṟu luvālāpāt tali
nilam paṟṟiyatu, maṟṟoṉṟu taṅkaṉīkā vaṭapuṟa ārāycci. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatiyē mutalil kaṇṭaṟiya liviṅ°ṭaṉ muṭivu ceytār. eṉavē °ṭāṉliyuṭaṉ  1871 ām āṇṭu navampar 16 il payaṇamāṉār. ‘avarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ nēriṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟi liruntu, ‘taṅkanīkā ērikkuḷ lusij’1 eṉṟa ōrāṟu pāynta tāyiṉum enta āṟum ataṉiṉiṉṟu veḷivaravilli’ eṉṟu uṇarntārkaḷ. ataṉpiṉ avarkaḷ ṭicampar 13 il ujji vantu cērntārkaḷ. 
°ṭāṉliyiṉ pārāṭṭu
ivviraṇṭu tiṅkaṭ kālattukkuḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉuṭaṉ °ṭāṉli naṉku paḻaka vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. pākap paḻaka liviṅ°ṭaṉiṭam avar koṇṭa naṭpum matippum, vaḷar
piṟipōl perukiṉa. “liviṅ°ṭaṉi otta vīramum, viṭāmuyaṟciyum, poṟumiyum, taṉṉalamaṟuppum, etiri
kaḷiṭiyum māṟāta aṉpum uṭiya paṇiyāḷari nāṉ kaṇṭatilli” eṉṟu avar maṉamārap pārāṭṭiṉār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tammi vañcittut tammītu kalleṟi
vōriyum etirttac cuṭuvatil viruppam illātavar. °ṭāṉli avvitam ceyvatiyum liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam aruṭ pārpiyāl taṭuttār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ kaṟuppu makkaḷ kaṇkaḷil veḷḷiyar uṇmi nākarika mēmpāṭṭukku ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tōṟṟiṉār,  veḷḷiyar kaṇkaḷilō ‘nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ oppaṟṟa naṇpar’ eṉat tōṟṟiṉār. 
poṟumikku iruppiṭam
taṅkaṉikā ērik karikaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷ, ataṉ ceḻitta kariyiṭiyē, nīril vāḻum mīṉpōlak kavaliyaṟṟu vāḻntiruntaṉar. pala iṭaṅkaḷil am makkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉ mutaliyōriṭam naṭpukkāṭṭi ēmāṟṟap pārttaṉar. āṉāl, nalla kālamāka liviṅ°ṭaṉ avarkaḷiṭamiruntu tappi akaṉṟaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil kariyilirunta makkaḷ liviṅ°ṭaṉik kariyēṟum paṭi tūṇṭiṉar. kariyēṟātatu kaṇṭu kalleṟintaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ appōtum ‘avarkaḷi etirkka vēṇṭā’ eṉṟu kūṟiṉarām. pala iṭaṅkaḷil arāpiyarkaḷum liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ vāymi, poṟumi, nakiccuvi ākiyavaṟṟil īṭupaṭṭut tam iyaṟkikkumāṟāka amitiyuṭaṉ naṭantu koṇṭaṉar. 
piriyā viṭi
ujjiyil liviṅ°ṭaṉ °ṭāṉliyuṭaṉ cila kālam iruntār. atu kāli kāli °ṭāṉli, “eñciya vēliyi ottivittat tāynāṭu ceṉṟu uṭalic
carippaṭuttikkoṇṭu piṉ ora kāl tirumpi varalām”eṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉukku vaṟpuṟuttiṉār. āṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ eppaṭiyāvatu eṭutta paṇiyi muṭittē ākavēṇṭum eṉṟu uṟutiyāka iruntār. eṉavē uṉāṉyempi 2 variyil ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa tam poruḷkaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu, tāy nāṭṭiliruntu varum kaṭitaṅkaḷiyum pārttuviṭṭu ataṉpiṉ vaṭakkē celvatu eṉpatu uṟutiyāyiṟṟu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ ippōtu ṭāṉliyiṉ viruntiṉarātalāl ivar ēṟic cella nalla kaḻuti oṉṟu (annāṭṭu vaḻakkap paṭi) aṉuppap paṭṭiruntatu. āṉāl, makkaṭ paṇiyāḷaruḷ ciṟantavarāṉa liviṅ°ṭaṉ ataṉi naṭakka viṭṭu, ellōriyum pōlat tāmum naṭantē vantār. aṅṅaṉam naṭantamiyāl kālellām koppuḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. itaṉāl liviṅ°ṭaṉ uṉāṉ
yempil cilanāḷ taṅkiṉār. aṅkē liviṅ°ṭaṉ poruḷkaḷiṟ cila koḷḷiyaṭikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cila, kariyāṉkaḷukku iriyāyiṉa. āyiṉum eñciyaviyē liviṅ°ṭaṉukku ērāḷamāṉatākat tōṉṟiṉa. naṇpa
riruvarum piriyā viṭi peṟṟatu kaṇkoḷḷāk kāṭci
yāka iruntatu. 
ariya kuṟippukaḷ
°ṭāṉli ceṉṟa cila mātaṅkaṭkup piṟakē avarāl ḵjāṉsipāriliruntu āṭkaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉ atuvariyil aṅkiruntu annāṭṭil kaṇṭa pala aruñceytikaḷic cuvipaṭac cittarittuvantār. avaṟṟuḷ annāṭṭukkoṭiya pāmpukaḷip pūṉi kolvati vivarikkum cittiram oṉṟākum. pāmpu aṟiyā vaṇṇam pūṉi patuṅkivantu pāmpiṉ paṭattil ōr aṟi aṟintu taliyi aḻuttik koṇṭu kaḻuttikkaṭittu eṟintuviṭum; piṉ tuṭituṭikkam uṭaliviṭṭup pōyviṭumām. pūṉiyiṉ ivvaruñ ceyalukkākavē ekiptiyar pūṉiyi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar eṉṟa avar karutiṉār. 
nāṭṭup piḷḷikaḷ
‘taṅkaṉīkāp pakutiyil nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē tāymār tam kuḻantikaḷi aṭimikaḷāka viṟṟaṉar’ eṉṟa ceyti airōppāvil kūṟappaṭṭu vantatu. iḵtu āḵprikarip paṟṟi airōppiyar iḻivāṉa eṇṇaṅ koḷvataṟkākavē parappap paṭṭatu. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tām kaṇṭavariyil, āḵprika uḷnāṭṭu makkaḷ koṭiya aṭimi vaṇikaṟ kippaṭṭaṉṟi ataṉi ātarikkavilli. avarkaḷum aṉpukkum ātaravukkum iṇaṅkum iyaṟkip paṇpu uṭiyavarkaḷē’ eṉṟu urittuḷḷār; mēlum “āḵprikap piḷḷikaḷukkup pōtiya nalla poḻutupōkku viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ illi;ittuṟiyit tirutta vēṇṭum” eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. liviṅ°ṭaṉ iṅkē uṇavu aṟṟavarkku uṇavum, uṭiyaṟṟavarkku uṭiyum, ātaravaṟṟa ciṟuvar ciṟumiyarkaḷukku ātaravum koṭuttu vantār eṉpatu ivar kuṟippukaḷāl terikiṟatu.
utavikku āṭkaḷ
1872ām āṇṭu āka°ṭu 25 il °ṭāṉli terin
teṭuttaṉuppiya 56 āṭkaḷum vantaṉar. “ivarkaḷ makō
lolōkkaḷip pōla vīramuṭiyavarkaḷ allarāyiṉum, avarkaḷip pōlaṉṟi uṇmiyum uḻippum uṭiya
varkaḷ” eṉṟu liviṅ°ṭaṉ eḻutiyuḷḷār. āṉāl piṉavarkaḷ ceyalkaḷiliruntu avarkaḷ vīrattilum kuṟintavarkaḷ allar eṉpatu terintatu. 
taḷarnta niliyilum pirayāṇam
ippayaṇattil ciṟitu tolivutāṉ liviṅ°ṭa
ṉāl tāmākac cellamuṭintatu. nōyālum taḷarcci
yālum naṭakkamuṭiyāta nilivantatu. eṉiṉum payaṇatti niṟutta maṉamiṉṟi liviṅ°ṭaṉ civikiyil payaṇam ceytār. civiki cellamuṭiyāta iṭattil ivar tam uṇmimikka paṇiyāṭkaḷāṉa sūsi, cumā ākiya
varkaḷāl tūkkic cellappaṭṭār. inniliyilum ivar tām kaṇṭavaṟṟiyum aṟintavaṟṟiyum tam kuṟippil eḻutat tavaṟavilli eṉpatu peruviyappēyākum. āṉāl ivar ēpral 23 mutal 26 vari oṉṟum eḻuta muṭiyāmal tēti maṭṭum kuṟittār. 27 il “nāṉ muṟṟilum taḷarn
tirukkiṟēṉ; pālāṭu vāṅkumpaṭi kūṟiyirukkiṟēṉ. mōlilāmō āṟṟiṉ 3 pakkam vantu cērntirukkiṟōm” eṉṟu eḻutiṉār.
liviṅ°ṭaṉ maṟivu
maṟunāṭ kāli mutal liviṅ°ṭaṉukkup pārvi kuṉṟiyatu. olikaḷik kēṭṭaṟiyum aṟivum kuṉṟiyatu aṉṟu iravu liviṅ°ṭaṉ vennīr kēṭṭār. eṉiṉum ataṉi muṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ tāṉ uṭkoḷḷa muṭintatu. kāli 4 maṇikkuk kaṭavuḷit toḻuta niliyilēyē liviṅ°ṭaṉ āvi iṟivaṉaṭi cārntatu. āḵprikāviṉ māperu naṇparākiya ivar  āḵprika maṇṇilēyē tam uṭal nīttār. 
uṭal aṭakkam
liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ paṇiyāṭkaḷ, liviṅ°ṭaṉ uyir nītta piṉpum ivar pakkam niṉṟu māṟātu pala tuṉpaṅkaḷit tāṅki ivar uṭalit tuṟimukam vari koṇṭuvantu cērttaṉar. liviṅ°ṭaṉirutayamum, kuṭalum jāṉsipāril aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉ uṭal ularttap paṭṭuk kappalil satāmpṭaṉ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu āṅkila nāṭṭār aṉivar kaṇṇīriṭiyēyum, pārāṭṭiṭiyēyum ve°ṭmiṉ°ṭar maṇṭapattil4 aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭatu. 
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Lusize             3.Rives Molilomo
2.	Unanembe       4.Westministerabbey


15. liviṅ°ṭaṉiṉ aruṅkuṇaṅkaḷ
perumikku mūlakāraṇaṅkaḷ
ulaka varalāṟṟil tam peyarip poṉ eḻuttuk
kaḷil poṟitta pukaḻuṭiya periyōr vāḻkki varalāṟu
kaḷi ellām ārāyntu pārttāl peṟappaṭavaṉa iviyākum. oṉṟu, avarkaḷ perumikkellām mūlakāraṇamāka iruppatu avarkaḷ koṇṭa uyar nōkkaṅkaḷum kuṟikkōḷkaḷum ākum. iraṇṭu, ak
kuṟikkōḷ vāḻkkiyil avarkaḷ tam tiṭṭatti evvaḷavu uyarvākat tīṭṭamuṭiyumō avvaḷavu  uyar
vākat tīṭṭiṉar eṉpatu neppōliyaṉ, nelsaṉ pōṉṟa periyār tam coṟokutiyil ‘muṭiyātatu’ eṉṟa col illi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭatu itaṉiyē yākum. liviṅ°ṭaṉ tam vāḻkkiyiṉ toṭakkattil tam viḷiyāṭṭilum ippaṇpik kāṭṭiṉār. ivar maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷ ēṟāta uyarattup pāṟi mītēṟit tam peyar poṟikka muyaṉṟār. vāḻkkip paṇiyilum ivar itē uyar pērārvam koṇṭiruntār eṉpatit tam iṟuti
